package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	events "bookmaker/events/internal"
	"bookmaker/pkg/store"
	"bookmaker/servers"
	"bookmaker/shared/dto"

	"github.com/joho/godotenv"
	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

func main() {
	if err := run(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// –≤—Å—è –ª–æ–≥–∏–∫–∞ –∑–¥–µ—Å—å, —á—Ç–æ–±—ã defer'—ã –≤—ã–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è–ª–∏—Å—å (log.Fatal –≤ main –∏—Ö –ø—Ä–æ–ø—É—Å–∫–∞–µ—Ç)
func run() error {
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer stop()

	if err := godotenv.Load(); err != nil {
		log.Printf("could not find .env, using defaults")
	}

	brokerURL, err := url.Parse(getenv("BROKER_URL", "amqp://guest:guest@localhost:5672/"))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parsing BROKER_URL: %w", err)
	}

	// –æ—Ç–¥–µ–ª—å–Ω—ã–µ —Å–æ–µ–¥–∏–Ω–µ–Ω–∏—è –¥–ª—è —á—Ç–µ–Ω–∏—è –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–æ–≤ –∏ –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–∫–∏ –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–æ–≤
	rabbitmq, err := connectWithRetry(ctx, brokerURL, 15, 2*time.Second, servers.ConnectRabbitmqBidi)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer closeLogged("rabbitmq connection", rabbitmq)

	// producer, err := connectWithRetry(ctx, brokerURL, 15, 2*time.Second, servers.ConnectRabbitmqProducer)
	// if err != nil {
	// 	return err
	// }
	// defer closeLogged("producer connection", producer)

	// –æ—Ç–∫—Ä—ã–≤–∞—é –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥–∏ –¥–ª—è –æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏—è —Å –æ—Å—Ç–∞–ª—å–Ω—ã–º–∏ –º–∏–∫—Ä–æ—Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–∞–º–∏
	requests, err := rabbitmq.OpenEventsRequestsAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening events requests channel: %w", err)
	}
	betsResponses, err := rabbitmq.OpenBetsResponsesAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening bets responses channel: %w", err)
	}
	gatewayResponses, err := rabbitmq.OpenGatewayResponsesAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening gateway responses channel: %w", err)
	}

	// –∏–Ω–∏—Ü–∏–∞–ª–∏–∑–∏—Ä—É—é —Ö—Ä–∞–Ω–∏–ª–∏—â–µ –¥–∞–Ω–Ω—ã—Ö
	st := store.NewMapStore[dto.Event]()
	st.Put(1, dto.Event{Id: 1, Coefficient: decimal.NewFromFloat(0.6), IsWin: false, IsTook: false})
	st.Put(2, dto.Event{Id: 2, Coefficient: decimal.NewFromFloat(0.2), IsWin: true, IsTook: true})
	st.Put(3, dto.Event{Id: 3, Coefficient: decimal.NewFromFloat(0.9), IsWin: false, IsTook: true})

	// —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞—é —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä
	server := events.NewServer(
		events.NewEventService(st),
		requests, betsResponses, gatewayResponses,
		func(err error) { log.Printf("events service error: %v", err) },
	)

	log.Printf(" [*] Events service is running. Waiting for requests on %q. Press CTRL+C to exit.", requests.Queue())

	// Run –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è –ø—Ä–∏ –æ—Ç–º–µ–Ω–µ ctx; –µ—Å–ª–∏ –∂–µ –ø–æ–¥–ø–∏—Å–∫–∞ –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–ª–∞—Å—å —Å–∞–º–∞
	// (–æ–±—Ä—ã–≤ —Å–æ–µ–¥–∏–Ω–µ–Ω–∏—è), ctx –∂–∏–≤ - —ç—Ç–æ –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞, –ø—É—Å—Ç—å –∫–æ–Ω—Ç–µ–π–Ω–µ—Ä –ø–µ—Ä–µ–∑–∞–ø—É—Å—Ç–∏—Ç—Å—è
	if err := server.Run(ctx); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("events server: %w", err)
	}
	if ctx.Err() == nil {
		return errors.New("events server stopped unexpectedly: consumer closed")
	}

	log.Println("Shutting down events service...")
	return nil
}

func connectWithRetry(
	ctx context.Context,
	u *url.URL,
	attempts int,
	delay time.Duration,
	connect func(context.Context, *url.URL) (*servers.RabbitmqClosable, error),
) (*servers.RabbitmqClosable, error) {
	var lastErr error

	for i := 1; i <= attempts; i++ {
		conn, err := connect(ctx, u)
		if err == nil {
			log.Println("Successfully connected to RabbitMQ")
			return conn, nil
		}

		lastErr = err
		log.Printf("Failed to connect to RabbitMQ (attempt %d/%d): %v", i, attempts, err)

		if i == attempts {
			break
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil, ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(delay):
		}
	}

	return nil, fmt.Errorf("could not connect to RabbitMQ after %d attempts: %w", attempts, lastErr)
}

func closeLogged(name string, c io.Closer) {
	if err := c.Close(); err != nil {
		log.Printf("%s close error: %v", name, err)
	}
}

func getenv(key, def string) string {
	if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
		return v
	}
	return def
}

package events

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sync"

	"bookmaker/channels"
	"bookmaker/messages"
	"bookmaker/pkg/genamqp"
	"bookmaker/proto/amqp"
	"bookmaker/schemas"
	"bookmaker/shared/dto"

	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

const (
	msgGetEvent            = "GetEventRequest"
	msgGetEventStatus      = "GetEventStatusRequest"
	msgCreateEvent         = "CreateEventRequest"
	msgSetEventStatus      = "SetEventStatusRequest"
	msgEvent               = "Event"
	msgEventStatusResponse = "EventStatusResponse"
	msgErrorResponse       = "ErrorResponse"

	statusOK       = "OK"
	statusNotFound = "NOT_FOUND"
	statusInternal = "INTERNAL_ERROR"
)

// ErrEventNotFound –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è, –µ—Å–ª–∏ —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å events –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∏–ª NOT_FOUND.
var ErrEventNotFound = errors.New("event not found")

type callResult struct {
	event  *schemas.Event
	status *schemas.EventStatusResponse
	code   string
	err    error
}

// EventClient –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã —á–µ—Ä–µ–∑ —Å–≥–µ–Ω–µ—Ä–∏—Ä–æ–≤–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–π AsyncAPI-–∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª
// events.requests –∏ –ø–æ–ª—É—á–∞–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—ã –∏–∑ bets.responses.
type EventClient struct {
	requests   *channels.EventsRequestsAMQP
	responses  *channels.BetsResponsesAMQP
	dispatcher *genamqp.Dispatcher

	mu      sync.Mutex
	pending map[string]chan callResult
}

func NewEventClient(
	requests *channels.EventsRequestsAMQP,
	responses *channels.BetsResponsesAMQP,
	onError func(error),
) *EventClient {
	if onError == nil {
		onError = func(error) {}
	}

	c := &EventClient{
		requests:  requests,
		responses: responses,
		pending:   make(map[string]chan callResult),
	}
	c.dispatcher = genamqp.NewDispatcher(onError)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgEvent, c.onEvent)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgEventStatusResponse, c.onEventStatusResponse)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgErrorResponse, c.onErrorResponse)
	return c
}

// Run —á–∏—Ç–∞–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—ã –æ—Ç events –∏ —Å–æ–ø–æ—Å—Ç–∞–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç –∏—Ö —Å –æ–∂–∏–¥–∞—é—â–∏–º–∏ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–∞–º–∏.
func (c *EventClient) Run(ctx context.Context) error {
	return c.dispatcher.Run(ctx, c.responses.Subscribe)
}

func (c *EventClient) GetEventStatus(ctx context.Context, eventID int) (dto.EventStatus, error) {
	eventID64 := int64(eventID)
	message := (&messages.GetEventStatusRequestOut{}).SetPayload(schemas.GetEventStatusRequest{
		EventID: &eventID64,
	})

	result, err := c.request(ctx, msgGetEventStatus, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
		return c.requests.SealGetEventStatusRequest(envelope, message)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("get event status from events: %w", err)
	}
	if result.code == statusNotFound {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("event with id %d: %w", eventID, ErrEventNotFound)
	}
	if result.err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("get event status from events: %w", result.err)
	}
	if result.code != statusOK {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("unexpected events reply status: %s", result.code)
	}
	if result.status == nil || result.status.Status == nil || *result.status.Status == "" {
		return "", errors.New("events reply has empty status")
	}

	return dto.EventStatus(*result.status.Status), nil
}

func (c *EventClient) GetEvent(ctx context.Context, eventID int) (*dto.Event, error) {
	eventID64 := int64(eventID)
	message := (&messages.GetEventRequestOut{}).SetPayload(schemas.GetEventRequest{
		EventID: &eventID64,
	})

	result, err := c.request(ctx, msgGetEvent, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
		return c.requests.SealGetEventRequest(envelope, message)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("get event from events: %w", err)
	}
	if result.code == statusNotFound {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("event with id %d: %w", eventID, ErrEventNotFound)
	}
	if result.err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("get event from events: %w", result.err)
	}
	if result.code != statusOK {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected events reply status: %s", result.code)
	}
	if result.event == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("events reply has empty event")
	}

	event, err := decodeEvent(*result.event)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode event response: %w", err)
	}
	return event, nil
}

func (c *EventClient) CreateEvent(
	ctx context.Context,
	coefficient decimal.Decimal,
) (*dto.Event, string, error) {
	coefficientString := coefficient.String()
	message := (&messages.CreateEventRequestOut{}).SetPayload(schemas.CreateEventRequest{
		Coefficient: &coefficientString,
	})

	result, err := c.request(ctx, msgCreateEvent, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
		return c.requests.SealCreateEventRequest(envelope, message)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, "", err
	}
	if result.err != nil {
		return nil, result.code, result.err
	}
	if result.code != statusOK {
		return nil, result.code, fmt.Errorf("unexpected events reply status: %s", result.code)
	}
	if result.event == nil {
		return nil, result.code, errors.New("events reply has empty event")
	}
	event, err := decodeEvent(*result.event)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, result.code, fmt.Errorf("decode event response: %w", err)
	}
	return event, result.code, nil
}

func (c *EventClient) SetEventStatus(
	ctx context.Context,
	eventID int,
	status dto.EventStatus,
) (*dto.EventStatusResponse, string, error) {
	eventID64 := int64(eventID)
	statusString := string(status)
	message := (&messages.SetEventStatusRequestOut{}).SetPayload(schemas.SetEventStatusRequest{
		EventID: &eventID64,
		Status:  &statusString,
	})

	result, err := c.request(ctx, msgSetEventStatus, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
		return c.requests.SealSetEventStatusRequest(envelope, message)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, "", err
	}
	if result.err != nil {
		return nil, result.code, result.err
	}
	if result.code != statusOK {
		return nil, result.code, fmt.Errorf("unexpected events reply status: %s", result.code)
	}
	if result.status == nil || result.status.EventID == nil || result.status.Status == nil {
		return nil, result.code, errors.New("events reply has incomplete event status")
	}

	return &dto.EventStatusResponse{
		EventID: int(*result.status.EventID),
		Status:  dto.EventStatus(*result.status.Status),
	}, result.code, nil
}

func (c *EventClient) request(
	ctx context.Context,
	messageType string,
	seal func(amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error,
) (callResult, error) {
	return c.call(ctx, func(ctx context.Context, correlationID string) error {
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, c.requests, seal, genamqp.Headers{
			MessageType:   messageType,
			CorrelationID: correlationID,
			ReplyTo:       c.responses.Queue(),
		})
	})
}

func (c *EventClient) call(
	ctx context.Context,
	publish func(context.Context, string) error,
) (callResult, error) {
	correlationID, err := newCorrelationID()
	if err != nil {
		return callResult{}, err
	}

	resultCh := make(chan callResult, 1)
	c.mu.Lock()
	c.pending[correlationID] = resultCh
	c.mu.Unlock()
	defer func() {
		c.mu.Lock()
		delete(c.pending, correlationID)
		c.mu.Unlock()
	}()

	if err := publish(ctx, correlationID); err != nil {
		return callResult{}, err
	}

	select {
	case result := <-resultCh:
		return result, nil
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return callResult{}, ctx.Err()
	}
}

func (c *EventClient) onEvent(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("event response has empty correlationId")
	}
	var message messages.EventIn
	if err := c.responses.UnsealEvent(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	payload := message.Payload()
	c.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{event: &payload, code: statusOK})
	return nil
}

func (c *EventClient) onEventStatusResponse(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("event status response has empty correlationId")
	}
	var message messages.EventStatusResponseIn
	if err := c.responses.UnsealEventStatusResponse(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	payload := message.Payload()
	c.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{status: &payload, code: statusOK})
	return nil
}

func (c *EventClient) onErrorResponse(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("event error response has empty correlationId")
	}
	var message messages.ErrorResponseIn
	if err := c.responses.UnsealErrorResponse(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	payload := message.Payload()
	code, text := stringValue(payload.Code), stringValue(payload.Message)
	if code == "" {
		code = statusInternal
	}
	if text == "" {
		text = code
	}
	c.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{code: code, err: errors.New(text)})
	return nil
}

func (c *EventClient) deliver(correlationID string, result callResult) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	resultCh := c.pending[correlationID]
	c.mu.Unlock()
	if resultCh == nil {
		return
	}
	select {
	case resultCh <- result:
	default:
	}
}

func decodeEvent(value schemas.Event) (*dto.Event, error) {
	if value.EventID == nil || value.Coefficient == nil || value.Status == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("event response is missing required fields")
	}
	coefficient, err := decimal.NewFromString(*value.Coefficient)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid coefficient: %w", err)
	}
	status := dto.EventStatus(*value.Status)
	return &dto.Event{
		Id:          int(*value.EventID),
		Coefficient: coefficient,
		IsWin:       status == dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN,
		IsTook:      status != dto.EVENT_STATUS_OPEN,
	}, nil
}

func newCorrelationID() (string, error) {
	var id [16]byte
	if _, err := rand.Read(id[:]); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return hex.EncodeToString(id[:]), nil
}

func stringValue(value *string) string {
	if value == nil {
		return ""
	}
	return *value
}

package internal

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"bookmaker/channels"
	"bookmaker/messages"
	"bookmaker/pkg/genamqp"
	"bookmaker/pkg/store"
	"bookmaker/proto/amqp"
	"bookmaker/schemas"
	"bookmaker/shared/dto"

	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

const (
	msgGetEvent       = "GetEventRequest"
	msgGetEventStatus = "GetEventStatusRequest"
	msgCreateEvent    = "CreateEventRequest"
	msgSetEventStatus = "SetEventStatusRequest"

	msgEvent               = "Event"
	msgEventStatusResponse = "EventStatusResponse"
	msgErrorResponse       = "ErrorResponse"

	codeNotFound    = "NOT_FOUND"
	codeValidation  = "VALIDATION_ERROR"
	codeEventClosed = "EVENT_CLOSED"
	codeInternal    = "INTERNAL_ERROR"
)

var errBadRequest = errors.New("bad request")

type replyChannel struct {
	out        genamqp.Publishing
	sealEvent  func(amqp.EnvelopeWriter, *messages.EventOut) error
	sealStatus func(amqp.EnvelopeWriter, *messages.EventStatusResponseOut) error
	sealError  func(amqp.EnvelopeWriter, *messages.ErrorResponseOut) error
}

func betsReply(channel *channels.BetsResponsesAMQP) *replyChannel {
	return &replyChannel{
		out: channel,
		sealEvent: func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter, m *messages.EventOut) error {
			return channel.SealEvent(e, m)
		},
		sealStatus: func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter, m *messages.EventStatusResponseOut) error {
			return channel.SealEventStatusResponse(e, m)
		},
		sealError: func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter, m *messages.ErrorResponseOut) error {
			return channel.SealErrorResponse(e, m)
		},
	}
}

func gatewayReply(channel *channels.GatewayResponsesAMQP) *replyChannel {
	return &replyChannel{
		out: channel,
		sealEvent: func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter, m *messages.EventOut) error {
			return channel.SealEvent(e, m)
		},
		sealStatus: func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter, m *messages.EventStatusResponseOut) error {
			return channel.SealEventStatusResponse(e, m)
		},
		sealError: func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter, m *messages.ErrorResponseOut) error {
			return channel.SealErrorResponse(e, m)
		},
	}
}

type response struct {
	event  *schemas.Event
	status *schemas.EventStatusResponse
	errMsg *schemas.ErrorResponse
	cause  error
}

func (r *replyChannel) send(ctx context.Context, correlationID string, resp response) error {
	headers := genamqp.Headers{CorrelationID: correlationID}

	switch {
	case resp.event != nil:
		message := (&messages.EventOut{}).SetPayload(*resp.event)
		headers.MessageType = msgEvent
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, r.out, func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return r.sealEvent(e, message)
		}, headers)
	case resp.status != nil:
		message := (&messages.EventStatusResponseOut{}).SetPayload(*resp.status)
		headers.MessageType = msgEventStatusResponse
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, r.out, func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return r.sealStatus(e, message)
		}, headers)
	case resp.errMsg != nil:
		message := (&messages.ErrorResponseOut{}).SetPayload(*resp.errMsg)
		headers.MessageType = msgErrorResponse
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, r.out, func(e amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return r.sealError(e, message)
		}, headers)
	default:
		return errors.New("empty response")
	}
}

// Server connects generated message types to event business logic.
type Server struct {
	svc          *EventService
	requests     *channels.EventsRequestsAMQP
	replies      map[string]*replyChannel
	gatewayQueue string
	onError      func(error)
	dispatcher   *genamqp.Dispatcher
}

func NewServer(
	svc *EventService,
	requests *channels.EventsRequestsAMQP,
	bets *channels.BetsResponsesAMQP,
	gateway *channels.GatewayResponsesAMQP,
	onError func(error),
) *Server {
	betsRoute, gatewayRoute := betsReply(bets), gatewayReply(gateway)
	gatewayQueue := gatewayRoute.out.Queue()

	s := &Server{
		svc:          svc,
		requests:     requests,
		replies:      map[string]*replyChannel{betsRoute.out.Queue(): betsRoute, gatewayQueue: gatewayRoute},
		gatewayQueue: gatewayQueue,
		onError:      onError,
		dispatcher:   genamqp.NewDispatcher(onError),
	}

	// Get requests may be sent by Bets or Gateway. Mutating requests reply only
	// to Gateway, as defined in the AsyncAPI contract.
	s.dispatcher.Handle(msgGetEvent, s.serve(true, s.onGetEvent))
	s.dispatcher.Handle(msgGetEventStatus, s.serve(true, s.onGetEventStatus))
	s.dispatcher.Handle(msgCreateEvent, s.serve(false, s.onCreateEvent))
	s.dispatcher.Handle(msgSetEventStatus, s.serve(false, s.onSetEventStatus))
	return s
}

func (s *Server) Run(ctx context.Context) error {
	return s.dispatcher.Run(ctx, s.requests.Subscribe)
}

func (s *Server) serve(allowBets bool, handle func(amqp.EnvelopeReader) response) genamqp.Handler {
	return func(ctx context.Context, envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader, meta genamqp.Meta) error {
		if meta.ReplyTo == "" {
			return errors.New("replyTo header is empty")
		}
		if !allowBets && meta.ReplyTo != s.gatewayQueue {
			return fmt.Errorf("replyTo %q is not allowed for this message", meta.ReplyTo)
		}
		route, ok := s.replies[meta.ReplyTo]
		if !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("unknown replyTo %q", meta.ReplyTo)
		}
		if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
			return errors.New("correlationId header is empty")
		}

		result := handle(envelope)
		if result.errMsg != nil && result.errMsg.Code != nil && *result.errMsg.Code == codeInternal && s.onError != nil {
			s.onError(fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", meta.MessageType, result.cause))
		}
		return route.send(ctx, meta.CorrelationID, result)
	}
}

func (s *Server) onGetEvent(envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) response {
	var msg messages.GetEventRequestIn
	if err := s.requests.UnsealGetEventRequest(envelope, &msg); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}
	id, err := requireID(msg.Payload().EventID)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	event, err := s.svc.GetEvent(id)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	return eventResponse(event)
}

func (s *Server) onGetEventStatus(envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) response {
	var msg messages.GetEventStatusRequestIn
	if err := s.requests.UnsealGetEventStatusRequest(envelope, &msg); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}
	id, err := requireID(msg.Payload().EventID)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	status, err := s.svc.GetEventStatus(id)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	return statusResponse(status)
}

func (s *Server) onCreateEvent(envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) response {
	var msg messages.CreateEventRequestIn
	if err := s.requests.UnsealCreateEventRequest(envelope, &msg); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}
	raw := msg.Payload().Coefficient
	if raw == nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: coefficient is required", errBadRequest))
	}
	coefficient, err := decimal.NewFromString(*raw)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: coefficient %q: %w", errBadRequest, *raw, err))
	}
	event, err := s.svc.CreateEvent(coefficient)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	return eventResponse(event)
}

func (s *Server) onSetEventStatus(envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) response {
	var msg messages.SetEventStatusRequestIn
	if err := s.requests.UnsealSetEventStatusRequest(envelope, &msg); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}
	payload := msg.Payload()
	id, err := requireID(payload.EventID)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	if payload.Status == nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: status is required", errBadRequest))
	}
	status, err := s.svc.SetEventStatus(id, dto.EventStatus(*payload.Status))
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	return statusResponse(status)
}

func requireID(value *int64) (int, error) {
	if value == nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("%w: event_id is required", errBadRequest)
	}
	return int(*value), nil
}

func eventResponse(event dto.Event) response {
	id, coefficient, status := int64(event.Id), event.Coefficient.String(), string(statusOf(event))
	return response{event: &schemas.Event{EventID: &id, Coefficient: &coefficient, Status: &status}}
}

func statusResponse(value dto.EventStatusResponse) response {
	id, status := int64(value.EventID), string(value.Status)
	return response{status: &schemas.EventStatusResponse{EventID: &id, Status: &status}}
}

func failure(err error) response {
	code, message := errorCode(err), err.Error()
	if code == codeInternal {
		message = "internal error"
	}
	return response{
		errMsg: &schemas.ErrorResponse{Code: &code, Message: &message},
		cause:  err,
	}
}

func errorCode(err error) string {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound):
		return codeNotFound
	case errors.Is(err, errBadRequest),
		errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidCoefficient),
		errors.Is(err, ErrUnexpectedEventStatus):
		return codeValidation
	case errors.Is(err, ErrEventCannotReopened):
		return codeEventClosed
	default:
		return codeInternal
	}
}
package internal

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"bookmaker/pkg/store"
	"bookmaker/shared/dto"

	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

func newTestService() *EventService {
	s := store.NewMapStore[dto.Event]()
	s.Put(1, dto.Event{Id: 1, Coefficient: decimal.NewFromFloat(0.6), IsWin: false, IsTook: false})
	s.Put(2, dto.Event{Id: 2, Coefficient: decimal.NewFromFloat(0.2), IsWin: true, IsTook: true})
	s.Put(3, dto.Event{Id: 3, Coefficient: decimal.NewFromFloat(0.9), IsWin: false, IsTook: true})

	return NewEventService(s)
}

func TestGetEvent(t *testing.T) {
	svc := newTestService()

	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		id      int
		wantErr error // nil - –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∏ –±—ã—Ç—å –Ω–µ –¥–æ–ª–∂–Ω–æ
	}{
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–∞–π–¥–µ–Ω–æ", 1, nil},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–µ –Ω–∞–π–¥–µ–Ω–æ", 99, store.ErrNotFound},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			event, err := svc.GetEvent(tt.id)

			if !errors.Is(err, tt.wantErr) {
				t.Errorf("err = %v, want %v", err, tt.wantErr)
			}
			if tt.wantErr == nil && event.Id != tt.id {
				t.Errorf("event.Id = %d, want %d", event.Id, tt.id)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestGetEventStatus(t *testing.T) {
	svc := newTestService()

	tests := []struct {
		name       string
		id         int
		wantStatus dto.EventStatus
		wantErr    error
	}{
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –µ—â—ë –Ω–µ –ø—Ä–æ–∏–∑–æ—à–ª–æ", 1, dto.EVENT_STATUS_OPEN, nil},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –ø—Ä–æ–∏–∑–æ—à–ª–æ, –≤—ã–∏–≥—Ä–∞–ª–æ", 2, dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN, nil},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –ø—Ä–æ–∏–∑–æ—à–ª–æ, –ø—Ä–æ–∏–≥—Ä–∞–ª–æ", 3, dto.EVENT_STATUS_LOSS, nil},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–µ –Ω–∞–π–¥–µ–Ω–æ", 99, "", store.ErrNotFound},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			reply, err := svc.GetEventStatus(tt.id)

			if !errors.Is(err, tt.wantErr) {
				t.Errorf("err = %v, want %v", err, tt.wantErr)
			}
			if tt.wantErr != nil {
				return
			}
			if reply.EventID != tt.id || reply.Status != tt.wantStatus {
				t.Errorf("reply = %+v, want event %d with status %q", reply, tt.id, tt.wantStatus)
			}
		})
	}
}

// failingStore –≤—Å–µ–≥–¥–∞ –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ—Ç –æ—à–∏–±–∫—É, –æ—Ç–ª–∏—á–Ω—É—é –æ—Ç store.ErrNotFound.
// –û—Å—Ç–∞–ª—å–Ω—ã–µ –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥—ã –∏–Ω—Ç–µ—Ä—Ñ–µ–π—Å–∞ –Ω–µ —Ä–µ–∞–ª–∏–∑–æ–≤–∞–Ω—ã: –ø—Ä–∏ –≤—ã–∑–æ–≤–µ –±—É–¥–µ—Ç –ø–∞–Ω–∏–∫–∞,
// –∞ —Ç–µ—Å—Ç–∏—Ä—É–µ–º—ã–º –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥–∞–º –æ–Ω–∏ –Ω–µ –Ω—É–∂–Ω—ã.
type failingStore struct {
	store.Store[dto.Event]
}

func (failingStore) Get(int) (dto.Event, error) {
	return dto.Event{}, errors.New("storage unavailable")
}

func TestStoreError(t *testing.T) {
	svc := NewEventService(failingStore{})

	t.Run("GetEvent", func(t *testing.T) {
		_, err := svc.GetEvent(1)
		if err == nil || errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
			t.Errorf("err = %v, want non-nil and not ErrNotFound", err)
		}
	})

	t.Run("GetEventStatus", func(t *testing.T) {
		_, err := svc.GetEventStatus(1)
		if err == nil || errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
			t.Errorf("err = %v, want non-nil and not ErrNotFound", err)
		}
	})
}

func TestCreateEvent(t *testing.T) {
	svc := newTestService()

	tests := []struct {
		name        string
		coefficient decimal.Decimal
		wantErr     error
	}{
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–Ω–æ", decimal.NewFromFloat(0.2), nil},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–µ —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–Ω–æ, –∫–æ—ç—Ñ 1", decimal.NewFromFloat(1), ErrInvalidCoefficient},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–µ —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–Ω–æ, –∫–æ—ç—Ñ 2", decimal.NewFromFloat(2), ErrInvalidCoefficient},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–µ —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–Ω–æ, –∫–æ—ç—Ñ 0", decimal.NewFromFloat(0), ErrInvalidCoefficient},
		{"—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–µ —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–Ω–æ, –∫–æ—ç—Ñ -1", decimal.NewFromFloat(-1), ErrInvalidCoefficient},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			event, err := svc.CreateEvent(tt.coefficient)

			if !errors.Is(err, tt.wantErr) {
				t.Errorf("err = %v, want %v", err, tt.wantErr)
			}
			if tt.wantErr != nil {
				return
			}
			if !event.Coefficient.Equal(tt.coefficient) {
				t.Errorf("event.Coefficient = %v, want %v", event.Coefficient, tt.coefficient)
			}

			stored, err := svc.Store.Get(event.Id)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("could not get event from store, err = %v", err)
			}
			if stored.Id != event.Id || stored.IsTook || stored.IsWin {
				t.Errorf("invalid event state in store: %+v", stored)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSetEventStatus(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name              string
		setupStore        func() store.Store[dto.Event]
		id                int
		status            dto.EventStatus
		wantErr           error
		checkStoreUpdated bool
	}{
		{
			name: "—É—Å–ø–µ—à–Ω–æ: —É—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∫–∞ —Å—Ç–∞—Ç—É—Å–∞ WIN",
			setupStore: func() store.Store[dto.Event] {
				s := store.NewMapStore[dto.Event]()
				s.Put(1, dto.Event{Id: 1})
				return s
			},
			id: 1, status: dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN, checkStoreUpdated: true,
		},
		{
			name: "—É—Å–ø–µ—à–Ω–æ: —É—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∫–∞ —Å—Ç–∞—Ç—É—Å–∞ LOSS",
			setupStore: func() store.Store[dto.Event] {
				s := store.NewMapStore[dto.Event]()
				s.Put(2, dto.Event{Id: 2})
				return s
			},
			id: 2, status: dto.EVENT_STATUS_LOSS, checkStoreUpdated: true,
		},
		{
			name: "–æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞: —Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –Ω–µ –Ω–∞–π–¥–µ–Ω–æ",
			setupStore: func() store.Store[dto.Event] {
				return store.NewMapStore[dto.Event]()
			},
			id: 99, status: dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN, wantErr: store.ErrNotFound,
		},
		{
			name: "–æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞: —Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ —É–∂–µ –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã—Ç–æ",
			setupStore: func() store.Store[dto.Event] {
				s := store.NewMapStore[dto.Event]()
				s.Put(3, dto.Event{Id: 3, IsTook: true})
				return s
			},
			id: 3, status: dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN, wantErr: ErrEventCannotReopened,
		},
		{
			name: "–æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞: –Ω–µ–æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–π —Å—Ç–∞—Ç—É—Å",
			setupStore: func() store.Store[dto.Event] {
				s := store.NewMapStore[dto.Event]()
				s.Put(4, dto.Event{Id: 4})
				return s
			},
			id: 4, status: "INVALID_STATUS", wantErr: ErrUnexpectedEventStatus,
		},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			svc := NewEventService(tt.setupStore())

			reply, err := svc.SetEventStatus(tt.id, tt.status)

			if !errors.Is(err, tt.wantErr) {
				t.Errorf("err = %v, want %v", err, tt.wantErr)
			}
			if tt.wantErr == nil && (reply.EventID != tt.id || reply.Status != tt.status) {
				t.Errorf("reply = %+v, want event %d with status %q", reply, tt.id, tt.status)
			}

			if tt.checkStoreUpdated {
				updated, getErr := svc.Store.Get(tt.id)
				if getErr != nil {
					t.Fatalf("failed to get updated event from store: %v", getErr)
				}
				if !updated.IsTook {
					t.Error("expected event.IsTook to be true")
				}
				if wantWin := tt.status == dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN; updated.IsWin != wantWin {
					t.Errorf("expected event.IsWin to be %v, got %v", wantWin, updated.IsWin)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}
package internal

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sync/atomic"

	"bookmaker/pkg/store"
	"bookmaker/shared/dto"

	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

var (
	ErrInvalidCoefficient    = errors.New("coefficient must be in range (0, 1)")
	ErrEventCannotReopened   = errors.New("event cannot be reopened")
	ErrUnexpectedEventStatus = errors.New("unexpected event status")
)

type EventService struct {
	Store       store.Store[dto.Event]
	lastEventID atomic.Int64 // TODO: –ø–æ–∫–∞ –Ω–µ—Ç –ë–î
}

func NewEventService(st store.Store[dto.Event]) *EventService {
	s := &EventService{Store: st}
	s.lastEventID.Store(9) // TODO: –ø–æ–∫–∞ –Ω–µ—Ç –ë–î
	return s
}

func statusOf(event dto.Event) dto.EventStatus {
	if !event.IsTook {
		return dto.EVENT_STATUS_OPEN
	}
	if event.IsWin {
		return dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN
	}
	return dto.EVENT_STATUS_LOSS
}

func (s *EventService) GetEvent(id int) (dto.Event, error) {
	event, err := s.Store.Get(id)
	if err != nil {
		return dto.Event{}, fmt.Errorf("get event %d: %w", id, err)
	}
	return event, nil
}

func (s *EventService) GetEventStatus(id int) (dto.EventStatusResponse, error) {
	event, err := s.GetEvent(id)
	if err != nil {
		return dto.EventStatusResponse{}, err
	}
	return dto.EventStatusResponse{EventID: event.Id, Status: statusOf(event)}, nil
}

func (s *EventService) CreateEvent(coefficient decimal.Decimal) (dto.Event, error) {
	if !coefficient.GreaterThan(decimal.Zero) || !coefficient.LessThan(decimal.NewFromInt(1)) {
		return dto.Event{}, ErrInvalidCoefficient
	}

	event := dto.Event{
		Id:          int(s.lastEventID.Add(1)),
		Coefficient: coefficient,
	}
	if err := s.Store.Put(event.Id, event); err != nil {
		return dto.Event{}, fmt.Errorf("put in store: %w", err)
	}
	return event, nil
}

func (s *EventService) SetEventStatus(id int, status dto.EventStatus) (dto.EventStatusResponse, error) {
	event, err := s.GetEvent(id)
	if err != nil {
		return dto.EventStatusResponse{}, err
	}
	if event.IsTook {
		return dto.EventStatusResponse{}, ErrEventCannotReopened
	}

	event.IsTook = true
	switch status {
	case dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN:
		event.IsWin = true
	case dto.EVENT_STATUS_LOSS:
		event.IsWin = false
	case dto.EVENT_STATUS_OPEN:
		// –∏–∑–º–µ–Ω–µ–Ω–∏–µ —Å—Ç–∞—Ç—É—Å–∞ –Ω–∞ EVENT_STATUS_OPEN –Ω–∏—á–µ–≥–æ –Ω–µ –º–µ–Ω—è–µ—Ç
	default:
		return dto.EventStatusResponse{}, ErrUnexpectedEventStatus
	}

	if err := s.Store.Put(event.Id, event); err != nil {
		return dto.EventStatusResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("put in store: %w", err)
	}
	return dto.EventStatusResponse{EventID: event.Id, Status: status}, nil
}
FROM golang:1.25-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app

COPY go.work go.work.sum go.mod go.sum ./
COPY pkg/broker/go.mod pkg/broker/go.sum ./pkg/broker/
COPY pkg/rmq/go.mod pkg/rmq/go.sum ./pkg/rmq/
COPY pkg/store/go.mod ./pkg/store/
COPY services/bets/go.mod services/bets/go.sum ./services/bets/
COPY services/events/go.mod services/events/go.sum ./services/events/
COPY services/gateway/go.mod services/gateway/go.sum ./services/gateway/
COPY shared/go.mod shared/go.sum ./shared/

ENV GOPROXY=https://goproxy.io,direct
RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/go/pkg/mod go work sync && go mod download

COPY . .
RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/go/pkg/mod go build -o /service ./services/bets/cmd/main.go

FROM alpine:latest
WORKDIR /
COPY --from=builder /service /service
ENTRYPOINT ["/service"]
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"bookmaker/bets/internal"
	"bookmaker/pkg/store"
	"bookmaker/servers"
	"bookmaker/shared/dto"

	"github.com/joho/godotenv"
	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

func main() {
	if err := run(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// –í—Å—è –ª–æ–≥–∏–∫–∞ –Ω–∞—Ö–æ–¥–∏—Ç—Å—è –≤ run, —á—Ç–æ–±—ã defer'—ã –≤—ã–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è–ª–∏—Å—å –¥–æ –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—Ç–∞ –≤ main.
func run() error {
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer stop()

	if err := godotenv.Load(); err != nil {
		log.Printf("could not find .env, using defaults")
	}

	brokerURL, err := url.Parse(getenv("BROKER_URL", "amqp://guest:guest@localhost:5672/"))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parsing BROKER_URL: %w", err)
	}

	rabbitmq, err := connectWithRetry(ctx, brokerURL, 15, 2*time.Second, servers.ConnectRabbitmqBidi)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("connecting to RabbitMQ: %w", err)
	}
	defer closeLogged("rabbitmq connection", rabbitmq)

	// –ò–º–µ–Ω–∞ –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥–µ–π –±–µ—Ä—É—Ç—Å—è –∏–∑ AsyncAPI-–∫–æ–Ω—Ç—Ä–∞–∫—Ç–∞.
	betsRequests, err := rabbitmq.OpenBetsRequestsAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening bets requests channel: %w", err)
	}
	defer closeLogged("bets requests channel", betsRequests)

	eventsRequests, err := rabbitmq.OpenEventsRequestsAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening events requests channel: %w", err)
	}
	defer closeLogged("events requests channel", eventsRequests)

	betsResponses, err := rabbitmq.OpenBetsResponsesAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening bets responses channel: %w", err)
	}
	defer closeLogged("bets responses channel", betsResponses)

	gatewayResponses, err := rabbitmq.OpenGatewayResponsesAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening gateway responses channel: %w", err)
	}
	defer closeLogged("gateway responses channel", gatewayResponses)

	// –ó–∞–≥–ª—É—à–µ—á–Ω–æ–µ —Ö—Ä–∞–Ω–∏–ª–∏—â–µ —Å—Ç–∞–≤–æ–∫, –∫–∞–∫ –∏ –≤ –ø—Ä–µ–∂–Ω–µ–π —Ä–µ–∞–ª–∏–∑–∞—Ü–∏–∏.
	st := store.NewMapStore[dto.Bet]()
	st.Put(1, dto.Bet{Id: 1, EventId: 1, Amount: decimal.NewFromFloat(1000.0), User: "–í—è—á–µ—Å–ª–∞–≤"})
	st.Put(2, dto.Bet{Id: 2, EventId: 1, Amount: decimal.NewFromFloat(250.50), User: "–ê–Ω–Ω–∞"})
	st.Put(3, dto.Bet{Id: 3, EventId: 2, Amount: decimal.NewFromFloat(15000.00), User: "–î–º–∏—Ç—Ä–∏–π"})
	st.Put(4, dto.Bet{Id: 4, EventId: 2, Amount: decimal.NewFromFloat(75.25), User: "–ú–∞—Ä–∏—è"})
	st.Put(5, dto.Bet{Id: 5, EventId: 3, Amount: decimal.NewFromFloat(4999.99), User: "–ò–≥–æ—Ä—å"})
	st.Put(6, dto.Bet{Id: 6, EventId: 3, Amount: decimal.NewFromFloat(12345.67), User: "–û–ª—å–≥–∞"})

	betService := internal.NewBetService(
		st,
		betsRequests,
		eventsRequests,
		betsResponses,
		gatewayResponses,
		func(err error) { log.Printf("bets service error: %v", err) },
	)
	// –°–Ω–∞—á–∞–ª–∞ –æ—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–∞–≤–ª–∏–≤–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å –∏ –¥–æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è –µ–≥–æ —Ñ–æ–Ω–æ–≤—ã—Ö —Ü–∏–∫–ª–æ–≤,
	// –∑–∞—Ç–µ–º –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–≤–∞—é—Ç—Å—è –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª—ã –∏ —Å–æ–µ–¥–∏–Ω–µ–Ω–∏–µ RabbitMQ.
	defer closeLogged("bet service", betService)

	errCh := make(chan error, 16)
	go func() {
		for err := range errCh {
			log.Printf("bet service error: %v", err)
		}
	}()

	if err := betService.Start(ctx, errCh); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("starting bet service: %w", err)
	}

	log.Printf(" [*] Bets service is running. Waiting for requests on %q. Press CTRL+C to exit.", betsRequests.Queue())
	<-ctx.Done()
	log.Println("Shutting down bet service...")
	return nil
}

func connectWithRetry(
	ctx context.Context,
	u *url.URL,
	attempts int,
	delay time.Duration,
	connect func(context.Context, *url.URL) (*servers.RabbitmqClosable, error),
) (*servers.RabbitmqClosable, error) {
	var lastErr error
	for i := 1; i <= attempts; i++ {
		conn, err := connect(ctx, u)
		if err == nil {
			log.Println("Successfully connected to RabbitMQ")
			return conn, nil
		}
		lastErr = err
		log.Printf("Failed to connect to RabbitMQ (attempt %d/%d): %v", i, attempts, err)
		if i == attempts {
			break
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil, ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(delay):
		}
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("could not connect to RabbitMQ after %d attempts: %w", attempts, lastErr)
}

func closeLogged(name string, c io.Closer) {
	if err := c.Close(); err != nil {
		log.Printf("%s close error: %v", name, err)
	}
}

func getenv(key, def string) string {
	if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
		return v
	}
	return def
}

package bets

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"sync"

	"bookmaker/channels"
	"bookmaker/messages"
	"bookmaker/pkg/genamqp"
	"bookmaker/proto/amqp"
	"bookmaker/schemas"

	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

const (
	msgGetBet         = "GetBetRequest"
	msgCreateBet      = "CreateBetRequest"
	msgBet            = "Bet"
	msgErrorResponse  = "ErrorResponse"
	statusOK          = "OK"
	msgGetBetDetails  = "GetBetDetailsRequest"
	msgBetDetails     = "BetDetails"
	msgGetBetsByEvent = "GetBetsByEventRequest"
	msgBetsByEvent    = "BetsByEvent"
	msgGetEvents      = "GetEventsRequest"
	msgEventsResponse = "EventsResponse"
)

type callResult struct {
	bet         *schemas.Bet
	status      string
	err         error
	betDetails  *schemas.BetDetails
	betsByEvent *schemas.BetsByEvent
	events      *schemas.EventsResponse
}

// BetClient –∏—Å–ø–æ–ª—å–∑—É–µ—Ç —Å–≥–µ–Ω–µ—Ä–∏—Ä–æ–≤–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–µ —Å–æ–æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏—è –∏ –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª—ã go-asyncapi.
type BetClient struct {
	requests       *channels.BetsRequestsAMQP
	responses      *channels.GatewayResponsesAMQP
	dispatcher     *genamqp.Dispatcher
	eventsRequests *channels.EventsRequestsAMQP

	mu      sync.Mutex
	pending map[string]chan callResult
}

func NewBetClient(
	requests *channels.BetsRequestsAMQP,
	eventsRequests *channels.EventsRequestsAMQP,
	responses *channels.GatewayResponsesAMQP,
	onError func(error),
) *BetClient {
	if onError == nil {
		onError = func(error) {}
	}

	c := &BetClient{
		requests:       requests,
		responses:      responses,
		pending:        make(map[string]chan callResult),
		eventsRequests: eventsRequests,
	}
	c.dispatcher = genamqp.NewDispatcher(onError)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgBet, c.onBet)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgErrorResponse, c.onErrorResponse)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgBetDetails, c.onBetDetails)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgBetsByEvent, c.onBetsByEvent)
	c.dispatcher.Handle(msgEventsResponse, c.onEventsResponse)
	return c
}

// Run —á–∏—Ç–∞–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—ã –∏–∑ gateway.responses –∏ —Å–æ–ø–æ—Å—Ç–∞–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç –∏—Ö —Å –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–∞–º–∏.
func (b *BetClient) Run(ctx context.Context) error {
	return b.dispatcher.Run(ctx, b.responses.Subscribe)
}

func (b *BetClient) GetEvents(
	ctx context.Context,
	status string,
	limit, offset int,
) (*schemas.EventsResponse, string, error) {

	request := schemas.GetEventsRequest{
		Limit:  limit,
		Offset: offset,
	}

	if status != "" {
		request.Status = status
	}

	message := (&messages.GetEventsRequestOut{}).SetPayload(request)

	return call(b, ctx, func(ctx context.Context, correlationID string) error {
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.eventsRequests, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.eventsRequests.SealGetEventsRequest(envelope, message)
		}, genamqp.Headers{
			MessageType:   msgGetEvents,
			CorrelationID: correlationID,
			ReplyTo:       b.responses.Queue(),
		})
	}, func(result callResult) (*schemas.EventsResponse, string, error) {
		return result.events, result.status, result.err
	})
}

func (b *BetClient) onEventsResponse(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("events response has empty correlationId")
	}

	var message messages.EventsResponseIn
	if err := b.responses.UnsealEventsResponse(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	payload := message.Payload()

	b.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{
		events: &payload,
		status: statusOK,
	})

	return nil
}

func (b *BetClient) GetBet(ctx context.Context, id int) (*schemas.Bet, string, error) {
	id64 := int64(id)
	message := (&messages.GetBetRequestOut{}).SetPayload(schemas.GetBetRequest{
		ID: &id64,
	})

	return call(b, ctx, func(ctx context.Context, correlationID string) error {
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.requests, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.requests.SealGetBetRequest(envelope, message)
		}, genamqp.Headers{
			MessageType:   msgGetBet,
			CorrelationID: correlationID,
			ReplyTo:       b.responses.Queue(),
		})
	}, func(result callResult) (*schemas.Bet, string, error) {
		return result.bet, result.status, result.err
	})
}

func (b *BetClient) GetBetsByEvent(
	ctx context.Context,
	eventID int,
) (*schemas.BetsByEvent, string, error) {
	eventID64 := int64(eventID)

	message := (&messages.GetBetsByEventRequestOut{}).SetPayload(
		schemas.GetBetsByEventRequest{
			EventID: &eventID64,
		},
	)

	return call(b, ctx, func(ctx context.Context, correlationID string) error {
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.requests, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.requests.SealGetBetsByEventRequest(envelope, message)
		}, genamqp.Headers{
			MessageType:   msgGetBetsByEvent,
			CorrelationID: correlationID,
			ReplyTo:       b.responses.Queue(),
		})
	}, func(result callResult) (*schemas.BetsByEvent, string, error) {
		return result.betsByEvent, result.status, result.err
	})
}

func (b *BetClient) CreateBet(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID, eventID int,
	amount decimal.Decimal,
) (*schemas.Bet, string, error) {
	userID64, eventID64 := int64(userID), int64(eventID)
	amountString := amount.String()
	message := (&messages.CreateBetRequestOut{}).SetPayload(schemas.CreateBetRequest{
		UserID:  &userID64,
		EventID: &eventID64,
		Amount:  &amountString,
	})

	return call(b, ctx, func(ctx context.Context, correlationID string) error {
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.requests, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.requests.SealCreateBetRequest(envelope, message)
		}, genamqp.Headers{
			MessageType:   msgCreateBet,
			CorrelationID: correlationID,
			ReplyTo:       b.responses.Queue(),
		})
	}, func(result callResult) (*schemas.Bet, string, error) {
		return result.bet, result.status, result.err
	})
}

func (b *BetClient) GetBetDetails(ctx context.Context, id int) (*schemas.BetDetails, string, error) {
	id64 := int64(id)
	message := (&messages.GetBetDetailsRequestOut{}).SetPayload(schemas.GetBetDetailsRequest{
		ID: &id64,
	})

	return call(b, ctx, func(ctx context.Context, correlationID string) error {
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.requests, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.requests.SealGetBetDetailsRequest(envelope, message)
		}, genamqp.Headers{
			MessageType:   msgGetBetDetails,
			CorrelationID: correlationID,
			ReplyTo:       b.responses.Queue(),
		})
	}, func(result callResult) (*schemas.BetDetails, string, error) {
		return result.betDetails, result.status, result.err
	})
}

func (b *BetClient) onBetDetails(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("bet details response has empty correlationId")
	}

	var message messages.BetDetailsIn
	if err := b.responses.UnsealBetDetails(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	payload := message.Payload()
	b.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{
		betDetails: &payload,
		status:     statusOK,
	})
	return nil
}

func call[T any](
	b *BetClient,
	ctx context.Context,
	publish func(context.Context, string) error,
	extract func(callResult) (*T, string, error),
) (*T, string, error) {
	correlationID, err := newCorrelationID()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, "", err
	}

	resultCh := make(chan callResult, 1)
	b.mu.Lock()
	b.pending[correlationID] = resultCh
	b.mu.Unlock()

	defer func() {
		b.mu.Lock()
		delete(b.pending, correlationID)
		b.mu.Unlock()
	}()

	if err := publish(ctx, correlationID); err != nil {
		return nil, "", err
	}

	select {
	case result := <-resultCh:
		return extract(result)
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, "", ctx.Err()
	}
}

func (b *BetClient) onBet(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("bet response has empty correlationId")
	}

	var message messages.BetIn
	if err := b.responses.UnsealBet(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	payload := message.Payload()
	b.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{
		bet:    &payload,
		status: statusOK,
	})
	return nil
}

func (b *BetClient) onErrorResponse(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("error response has empty correlationId")
	}

	var message messages.ErrorResponseIn
	if err := b.responses.UnsealErrorResponse(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	payload := message.Payload()
	status := stringValue(payload.Code)
	text := stringValue(payload.Message)
	if status == "" {
		status = "INTERNAL_ERROR"
	}
	if text == "" {
		text = status
	}

	b.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{
		status: status,
		err:    errors.New(text),
	})
	return nil
}

func (b *BetClient) onBetsByEvent(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
	meta genamqp.Meta,
) error {
	if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
		return errors.New("bets by event response has empty correlationId")
	}

	var message messages.BetsByEventIn
	if err := b.responses.UnsealBetsByEvent(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	payload := message.Payload()

	b.deliver(meta.CorrelationID, callResult{
		betsByEvent: &payload,
		status:      statusOK,
	})

	return nil
}

func (b *BetClient) deliver(correlationID string, result callResult) {
	b.mu.Lock()
	resultCh := b.pending[correlationID]
	b.mu.Unlock()
	if resultCh == nil {
		return // –ó–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å —É–∂–µ –∑–∞–≤–µ—Ä—à–∏–ª—Å—è –ø–æ —Ç–∞–π–º–∞—É—Ç—É –∏–ª–∏ –æ—Ç–º–µ–Ω–µ –∫–æ–Ω—Ç–µ–∫—Å—Ç–∞.
	}

	select {
	case resultCh <- result:
	default:
	}
}

func newCorrelationID() (string, error) {
	var id [16]byte
	if _, err := rand.Read(id[:]); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return hex.EncodeToString(id[:]), nil
}

func stringValue(value *string) string {
	if value == nil {
		return ""
	}
	return *value
}

require (

package internal

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"bookmaker/channels"
	"bookmaker/events"
	"bookmaker/messages"
	"bookmaker/pkg/genamqp"
	"bookmaker/pkg/store"
	"bookmaker/proto/amqp"
	"bookmaker/schemas"
	"bookmaker/shared/dto"

	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

const (
	eventsCallTimeout = 5 * time.Second

	msgGetBet        = "GetBetRequest"
	msgCreateBet     = "CreateBetRequest"
	msgBet           = "Bet"
	msgErrorResponse = "ErrorResponse"

	codeNotFound    = "NOT_FOUND"
	codeValidation  = "VALIDATION_ERROR"
	codeEventClosed = "EVENT_CLOSED"
	codeInternal    = "INTERNAL_ERROR"

	msgGetBetDetails = "GetBetDetailsRequest"
	msgBetDetails    = "BetDetails"

	msgGetBetsByEvent = "GetBetsByEventRequest"
	msgBetsByEvent    = "BetsByEvent"
)

var (
	errBadRequest  = errors.New("bad request")
	errEventClosed = errors.New("event is closed or does not accept bets")
)

type response struct {
	bet         *schemas.Bet
	errMsg      *schemas.ErrorResponse
	cause       error
	betDetails  *schemas.BetDetails
	betsByEvent *schemas.BetsByEvent
}

// BetService —Å–æ–¥–µ—Ä–∂–∏—Ç –±–∏–∑–Ω–µ—Å-–ª–æ–≥–∏–∫—É —Å—Ç–∞–≤–æ–∫ –∏ —Å–≤—è–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç –µ—ë —Å–æ —Å–≥–µ–Ω–µ—Ä–∏—Ä–æ–≤–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–º–∏
// AsyncAPI-–∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª–∞–º–∏. –°—Ç–∞—Ä—ã–π RPCClient/RPCServer –∑–¥–µ—Å—å –±–æ–ª—å—à–µ –Ω–µ –∏—Å–ø–æ–ª—å–∑—É—é—Ç—Å—è.
type BetService struct {
	Store store.Store[dto.Bet]

	requests         *channels.BetsRequestsAMQP
	gatewayResponses *channels.GatewayResponsesAMQP
	EventClient      *events.EventClient

	dispatcher *genamqp.Dispatcher
	onError    func(error)

	lastBetID atomic.Int64

	startMu      sync.Mutex
	cancelServer context.CancelFunc
	cancelClient context.CancelFunc
	started      bool
	wg           sync.WaitGroup
}

func NewBetService(
	betStore store.Store[dto.Bet],
	requests *channels.BetsRequestsAMQP,
	eventsRequests *channels.EventsRequestsAMQP,
	betsResponses *channels.BetsResponsesAMQP,
	gatewayResponses *channels.GatewayResponsesAMQP,
	onError func(error),
) *BetService {
	if onError == nil {
		onError = func(error) {}
	}

	b := &BetService{
		Store:            betStore,
		requests:         requests,
		gatewayResponses: gatewayResponses,
		onError:          onError,
		dispatcher:       genamqp.NewDispatcher(onError),
	}
	b.EventClient = events.NewEventClient(eventsRequests, betsResponses, onError)
	b.lastBetID.Store(6) // –ø–æ—Å–ª–µ–¥–Ω—è—è —Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫–∞ –≤ –∑–∞–≥–ª—É—à–µ—á–Ω–æ–º —Ö—Ä–∞–Ω–∏–ª–∏—â–µ

	b.dispatcher.Handle(msgGetBet, b.serve(b.onGetBet))
	b.dispatcher.Handle(msgCreateBet, b.serve(b.onCreateBet))
	b.dispatcher.Handle(msgGetBetDetails, b.serve(b.onGetBetDetails))
	b.dispatcher.Handle(msgGetBetsByEvent, b.serve(b.onGetBetsByEvent))
	return b
}

// Start –∑–∞–ø—É—Å–∫–∞–µ—Ç –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–æ–≤ Bets –∏ –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç, –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞—é—â–∏–π –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—ã Events.
func (b *BetService) Start(ctx context.Context, errCh chan<- error) error {
	b.startMu.Lock()
	if b.started {
		b.startMu.Unlock()
		return errors.New("bet service already started")
	}
	serverCtx, cancelServer := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	clientCtx, cancelClient := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	b.cancelServer = cancelServer
	b.cancelClient = cancelClient
	b.started = true
	b.wg.Add(2)
	b.startMu.Unlock()

	// –ö–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç Events –∂–∏–≤—ë—Ç –¥–æ–ª—å—à–µ –ø—Ä–∏—ë–º–Ω–∏–∫–∞ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–æ–≤: —É–∂–µ –Ω–∞—á–∞—Ç–∞—è –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç–∫–∞
	// CreateBet –º–æ–∂–µ—Ç –ø–æ–ª—É—á–∏—Ç—å –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç Events –≤–æ –≤—Ä–µ–º—è graceful shutdown.
	go func() {
		defer b.wg.Done()
		err := b.EventClient.Run(clientCtx)
		if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
			reportError(errCh, fmt.Errorf("events response client: %w", err))
		} else if clientCtx.Err() == nil {
			reportError(errCh, errors.New("events response client stopped unexpectedly"))
		}
	}()

	go func() {
		defer b.wg.Done()
		err := b.dispatcher.Run(serverCtx, b.requests.Subscribe)
		if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
			reportError(errCh, fmt.Errorf("bets request server: %w", err))
		} else if serverCtx.Err() == nil {
			reportError(errCh, errors.New("bets request server stopped unexpectedly: consumer closed"))
		}
		cancelClient()
	}()

	return nil
}

// Close –æ—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–∞–≤–ª–∏–≤–∞–µ—Ç —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä, –∑–∞—Ç–µ–º –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç Events, –∏ –¥–æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è —Ñ–æ–Ω–æ–≤—ã—Ö —Ü–∏–∫–ª–æ–≤.
func (b *BetService) Close() error {
	b.startMu.Lock()
	cancelServer, cancelClient, started := b.cancelServer, b.cancelClient, b.started
	b.startMu.Unlock()
	if cancelServer != nil {
		cancelServer()
	}
	if !started {
		return nil
	}
	b.wg.Wait()
	if cancelClient != nil {
		cancelClient()
	}
	return nil
}

func (b *BetService) serve(handle func(context.Context, amqp.EnvelopeReader) response) genamqp.Handler {
	return func(ctx context.Context, envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader, meta genamqp.Meta) error {
		if meta.ReplyTo == "" {
			return errors.New("replyTo header is empty")
		}
		if meta.ReplyTo != b.gatewayResponses.Queue() {
			return fmt.Errorf("replyTo %q is not allowed for Bets", meta.ReplyTo)
		}
		if meta.CorrelationID == "" {
			return errors.New("correlationId header is empty")
		}

		result := handle(ctx, envelope)
		if result.errMsg != nil && result.errMsg.Code != nil && *result.errMsg.Code == codeInternal && b.onError != nil {
			b.onError(fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", meta.MessageType, result.cause))
		}
		return b.send(ctx, meta.CorrelationID, result)
	}
}

func (b *BetService) send(ctx context.Context, correlationID string, result response) error {
	headers := genamqp.Headers{CorrelationID: correlationID}

	switch {
	case result.bet != nil:
		message := (&messages.BetOut{}).SetPayload(*result.bet)
		headers.MessageType = msgBet
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.gatewayResponses, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.gatewayResponses.SealBet(envelope, message)
		}, headers)
	case result.errMsg != nil:
		message := (&messages.ErrorResponseOut{}).SetPayload(*result.errMsg)
		headers.MessageType = msgErrorResponse
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.gatewayResponses, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.gatewayResponses.SealErrorResponse(envelope, message)
		}, headers)
	case result.betDetails != nil:
		message := (&messages.BetDetailsOut{}).SetPayload(*result.betDetails)
		headers.MessageType = msgBetDetails
		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.gatewayResponses, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.gatewayResponses.SealBetDetails(envelope, message)
		}, headers)
	case result.betsByEvent != nil:
		message := (&messages.BetsByEventOut{}).SetPayload(*result.betsByEvent)
		headers.MessageType = msgBetsByEvent

		return genamqp.Publish(ctx, b.gatewayResponses, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error {
			return b.gatewayResponses.SealBetsByEvent(envelope, message)
		}, headers)
	default:
		return errors.New("empty response")
	}
}

func (b *BetService) onGetBetDetails(ctx context.Context, envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) response {
	var message messages.GetBetDetailsRequestIn
	if err := b.requests.UnsealGetBetDetailsRequest(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}
	id := message.Payload().ID
	if id == nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: id is required", errBadRequest))
	}

	bet, err := b.Store.Get(int(*id))
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}

	eventCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, eventsCallTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	event, err := b.EventClient.GetEvent(eventCtx, bet.EventId)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("get event for bet %d: %w", bet.Id, err))
	}

	return betDetailsResponse(bet, *event)
}

func (b *BetService) onGetBet(_ context.Context, envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) response {
	var message messages.GetBetRequestIn
	if err := b.requests.UnsealGetBetRequest(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}
	id := message.Payload().ID
	if id == nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: id is required", errBadRequest))
	}

	bet, err := b.Store.Get(int(*id))
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	return betResponse(bet)
}

func (b *BetService) onGetBetsByEvent(
	_ context.Context,
	envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader,
) response {
	var message messages.GetBetsByEventRequestIn

	if err := b.requests.UnsealGetBetsByEventRequest(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}

	eventID := message.Payload().EventID
	if eventID == nil || *eventID <= 0 {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: event_id must be positive", errBadRequest))
	}

	bets, err := b.Store.FindBy(func(bet dto.Bet) bool {
		return bet.EventId == int(*eventID)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("find bets for event %d: %w", *eventID, err))
	}

	resultBets := make([]schemas.Bet, 0, len(bets))
	for _, bet := range bets {
		result := betResponse(bet)
		if result.bet != nil {
			resultBets = append(resultBets, *result.bet)
		}
	}

	eventIDValue := *eventID

	return response{
		betsByEvent: &schemas.BetsByEvent{
			EventID: &eventIDValue,
			Bets:    &resultBets,
		},
	}
}

func (b *BetService) onCreateBet(_ context.Context, envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) response {
	var message messages.CreateBetRequestIn
	if err := b.requests.UnsealCreateBetRequest(envelope, &message); err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}
	payload := message.Payload()
	if payload.UserID == nil || payload.EventID == nil || payload.Amount == nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: user_id, event_id and amount are required", errBadRequest))
	}
	amount, err := decimal.NewFromString(*payload.Amount)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid amount: %w", errBadRequest, err))
	}

	bet, err := b.createBet(int(*payload.UserID), int(*payload.EventID), amount)
	if err != nil {
		return failure(err)
	}
	return betResponse(bet)
}

func (b *BetService) createBet(userID, eventID int, amount decimal.Decimal) (dto.Bet, error) {
	// –û–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–∞ –º–æ–∂–µ—Ç –≤—ã–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è—Ç—å—Å—è –¥–æ–ª—å—à–µ –æ–¥–Ω–æ–≥–æ —Å–æ–æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏—è; –Ω–∞ –≤—ã–∑–æ–≤ Events
	// –æ–≥—Ä–∞–Ω–∏—á–∏–≤–∞–µ–º –æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–Ω–∏–µ –ø—è—Ç—å—é —Å–µ–∫—É–Ω–¥–∞–º–∏, –∫–∞–∫ –∏ –≤ –ø—Ä–µ–∂–Ω–µ–º RPC-–∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç–µ.
	eventCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), eventsCallTimeout)
	defer cancel()

	isOpen, err := b.isEventOpen(eventCtx, eventID)
	if err != nil {
		return dto.Bet{}, fmt.Errorf("validation failed: %w", err)
	}
	if !isOpen {
		return dto.Bet{}, errEventClosed
	}

	bet := dto.Bet{
		Id:      int(b.lastBetID.Add(1)),
		EventId: eventID,
		Amount:  amount,
		User:    fmt.Sprintf("User_%d", userID),
	}
	// –°–æ—Ö—Ä–∞–Ω—è–µ–º –ø—Ä–µ–∂–Ω–µ–µ –ø–æ–≤–µ–¥–µ–Ω–∏–µ: –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∏ –≤—Ä–µ–º–µ–Ω–Ω–æ–≥–æ MapStore –Ω–µ –º–µ–Ω—è—é—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç—É.
	_ = b.Store.Put(bet.Id, bet)
	return bet, nil
}

func (b *BetService) isEventOpen(ctx context.Context, eventID int) (bool, error) {
	status, err := b.EventClient.GetEventStatus(ctx, eventID)
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get event status: %w", err)
	}
	return status == dto.EVENT_STATUS_OPEN, nil
}

func betResponse(bet dto.Bet) response {
	id, eventID := int64(bet.Id), int64(bet.EventId)
	amount, user := bet.Amount.String(), bet.User
	return response{bet: &schemas.Bet{
		ID:      &id,
		EventID: &eventID,
		Amount:  &amount,
		User:    &user,
	}}
}

func betDetailsResponse(bet dto.Bet, event dto.Event) response {
	eventID := int64(event.Id)
	coefficient := event.Coefficient.String()
	status := string(eventStatus(event))

	return response{betDetails: &schemas.BetDetails{
		Bet: betSchema(bet),
		Event: &schemas.Event{
			EventID:     &eventID,
			Coefficient: &coefficient,
			Status:      &status,
		},
	}}
}

func betSchema(bet dto.Bet) *schemas.Bet {
	id, eventID := int64(bet.Id), int64(bet.EventId)
	amount, user := bet.Amount.String(), bet.User
	return &schemas.Bet{
		ID:      &id,
		EventID: &eventID,
		Amount:  &amount,
		User:    &user,
	}
}

func eventStatus(event dto.Event) dto.EventStatus {
	if !event.IsTook {
		return dto.EVENT_STATUS_OPEN
	}
	if event.IsWin {
		return dto.EVENT_STATUS_WIN
	}
	return dto.EVENT_STATUS_LOSS
}

func failure(err error) response {
	code, message := errorCode(err), err.Error()
	if code == codeInternal {
		message = "internal error"
	}
	return response{
		errMsg: &schemas.ErrorResponse{Code: &code, Message: &message},
		cause:  err,
	}
}

func errorCode(err error) string {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound), errors.Is(err, events.ErrEventNotFound):
		return codeNotFound
	case errors.Is(err, errBadRequest):
		return codeValidation
	case errors.Is(err, errEventClosed):
		return codeEventClosed
	default:
		return codeInternal
	}
}

func reportError(errCh chan<- error, err error) {
	if errCh == nil || err == nil {
		return
	}
	select {
	case errCh <- err:
	default:
	}
}

package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"bookmaker/bets"
	gateway "bookmaker/gateway/internal"
	"bookmaker/servers"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
	"github.com/joho/godotenv"
)

func main() {
	if err := run(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// –í—Å—è –ª–æ–≥–∏–∫–∞ –Ω–∞—Ö–æ–¥–∏—Ç—Å—è –∑–¥–µ—Å—å, —á—Ç–æ–±—ã defer'—ã –≤—ã–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è–ª–∏—Å—å (log.Fatal –≤ main –∏—Ö –ø—Ä–æ–ø—É—Å–∫–∞–µ—Ç).
func run() error {
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer stop()

	if err := godotenv.Load(); err != nil {
		log.Printf("could not find .env, using defaults")
	}

	brokerURL, err := url.Parse(getenv("BROKER_URL", "amqp://guest:guest@localhost:5672/"))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parsing BROKER_URL: %w", err)
	}

	rabbitmq, err := connectWithRetry(ctx, brokerURL, 15, 2*time.Second, servers.ConnectRabbitmqBidi)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer closeLogged("rabbitmq connection", rabbitmq)

	// –°–≥–µ–Ω–µ—Ä–∏—Ä–æ–≤–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–µ –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª—ã –¥–ª—è –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–∫–∏ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–æ–≤ –∏ –ø–æ–ª—É—á–µ–Ω–∏—è –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–æ–≤.
	betsRequests, err := rabbitmq.OpenBetsRequestsAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening bets requests channel: %w", err)
	}

	gatewayResponses, err := rabbitmq.OpenGatewayResponsesAMQP(ctx, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("opening gateway responses channel: %w", err)
	}

	betClient := bets.NewBetClient(betsRequests, gatewayResponses, func(err error) {
		log.Printf("bet client error: %v", err)
	})

	// –ö–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç —Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç–∞–µ—Ç –¥–æ –∑–∞–≤–µ—Ä—à–µ–Ω–∏—è HTTP shutdown, —á—Ç–æ–±—ã —É—Å–ø–µ—Ç—å –ø–æ–ª—É—á–∏—Ç—å –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—ã
	// –Ω–∞ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã, –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–µ —É–∂–µ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–∞—Ç—ã–≤–∞—é—Ç—Å—è.
	clientCtx, stopClient := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	clientDone := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(clientDone)
		if err := betClient.Run(clientCtx); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
			log.Printf("bet client stopped: %v", err)
		}
	}()
	defer func() {
		stopClient()
		<-clientDone
	}()

	r := gin.Default()
	gw := gateway.NewGateway(r, betClient)
	gw.SetupRouter()

	srv := &http.Server{
		Addr:    ":8080",
		Handler: r,
	}

	srvErr := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		log.Println("Server started on :8080")
		if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
			srvErr <- err
		}
	}()

	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		log.Println("Shutting down gracefully...")
	case err := <-srvErr:
		return fmt.Errorf("server error: %w", err)
	}

	shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if err := srv.Shutdown(shutdownCtx); err != nil {
		log.Printf("Server forced to shutdown: %v", err)
	}

	log.Println("Server exited")
	return nil
}

func connectWithRetry(
	ctx context.Context,
	u *url.URL,
	attempts int,
	delay time.Duration,
	connect func(context.Context, *url.URL) (*servers.RabbitmqClosable, error),
) (*servers.RabbitmqClosable, error) {
	var lastErr error
	for i := 1; i <= attempts; i++ {
		conn, err := connect(ctx, u)
		if err == nil {
			log.Println("Successfully connected to RabbitMQ")
			return conn, nil
		}
		lastErr = err
		log.Printf("Failed to connect to RabbitMQ (attempt %d/%d): %v", i, attempts, err)
		if i == attempts {
			break
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil, ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(delay):
		}
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("could not connect to RabbitMQ after %d attempts: %w", attempts, lastErr)
}

func closeLogged(name string, c io.Closer) {
	if err := c.Close(); err != nil {
		log.Printf("%s close error: %v", name, err)
	}
}

func getenv(key, def string) string {
	if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
		return v
	}
	return def
}

package internal

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"bookmaker/bets"

	"github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
	"github.com/shopspring/decimal"
)

type Gateway struct {
	BetClient *bets.BetClient
	Router    *gin.Engine
}

func NewGateway(router *gin.Engine, betClient *bets.BetClient) *Gateway {
	return &Gateway{
		BetClient: betClient,
		Router:    router,
	}
}

func (g *Gateway) SetupRouter() {
	// TODO: –∫–æ–≥–¥–∞ –±—É–¥–µ—Ç –±–æ–ª—å—à–µ —Ä–æ—É—Ç–æ–≤ –Ω—É–∂–Ω–æ –±—É–¥–µ—Ç —Å—Ñ–æ—Ä–º–∏—Ä–æ–≤–∞—Ç—å –≥—Ä—É–ø–ø—ã.
	g.Router.LoadHTMLGlob("/app/templates/*.html")
	g.Router.GET("/", g.GetIndex)
	g.Router.GET("/bet/:id", g.GetBet)
	g.Router.POST("/bet", g.CreateBet)
	g.Router.GET("/bet/:id/details", g.GetBetDetails)
	g.Router.GET("/event/:id/bets", g.GetBetsByEvent)
	g.Router.GET("/events", g.GetEvents)
}

func (g *Gateway) Run(addr string) error {
	return g.Router.Run(addr)
}

func (g *Gateway) GetIndex(c *gin.Context) {
	c.HTML(http.StatusOK, "index.html", nil)
}

func (g *Gateway) GetEvents(c *gin.Context) {
	// –ü—Ä–æ–≤–µ—Ä—è–µ–º —Ñ–∏–ª—å—Ç—Ä –ø–æ —Å—Ç–∞—Ç—É—Å—É.
	statusFilter := c.Query("status")
	switch statusFilter {
	case "", "open", "win", "loss":
		// –î–æ–ø—É—Å—Ç–∏–º–æ–µ –∑–Ω–∞—á–µ–Ω–∏–µ.
	default:
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{
			"error": "invalid status: expected open, win or loss",
		})
		return
	}

	// –†–∞–∑–º–µ—Ä —Å—Ç—Ä–∞–Ω–∏—Ü—ã –ø–æ —É–º–æ–ª—á–∞–Ω–∏—é ‚Äî 20.
	limit := 20
	if rawLimit := c.Query("limit"); rawLimit != "" {
		parsedLimit, err := strconv.Atoi(rawLimit)
		if err != nil || parsedLimit < 1 || parsedLimit > 100 {
			c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{
				"error": "limit must be between 1 and 100",
			})
			return
		}
		limit = parsedLimit
	}

	// –°–º–µ—â–µ–Ω–∏–µ –ø–æ —É–º–æ–ª—á–∞–Ω–∏—é ‚Äî 0.
	offset := 0
	if rawOffset := c.Query("offset"); rawOffset != "" {
		parsedOffset, err := strconv.Atoi(rawOffset)
		if err != nil || parsedOffset < 0 {
			c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{
				"error": "offset must be a non-negative integer",
			})
			return
		}
		offset = parsedOffset
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(c.Request.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	result, status, err := g.BetClient.GetEvents(
		ctx,
		statusFilter,
		limit,
		offset,
	)

	if err != nil && status == "" {
		code := http.StatusBadGateway
		if errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
			code = http.StatusGatewayTimeout
		}

		c.JSON(code, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
		return
	}

	switch status {
	case "OK":
		if result == nil {
			c.JSON(http.StatusBadGateway, gin.H{
				"error": "events service returned an empty response",
			})
			return
		}
		c.JSON(http.StatusOK, result)

	case "VALIDATION_ERROR":
		message := "invalid events request"
		if err != nil {
			message = err.Error()
		}
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": message})

	default:
		message := "internal error"
		if err != nil {
			message = err.Error()
		}
		c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": message})
	}
}

func (g *Gateway) GetBet(c *gin.Context) {
	rawID := c.Param("id")
	id, err := strconv.Atoi(rawID)
	if err != nil {
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "bad id"})
		return
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(c.Request.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	bet, status, err := g.BetClient.GetBet(ctx, id)
	if err != nil && status == "" {
		code := http.StatusBadGateway
		if errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
			code = http.StatusGatewayTimeout
		}
		c.JSON(code, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
		return
	}

	switch status {
	case "OK":
		c.JSON(http.StatusOK, bet)
	case "NOT_FOUND":
		c.JSON(http.StatusNotFound, gin.H{"error": "bet not found"})
	case "VALIDATION_ERROR":
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
	default: // INTERNAL_ERROR –∏ –ø—Ä–æ—á–∏–µ.
		c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
	}
}

type CreateBetHTTPReq struct {
	UserID  int    `json:"user_id" binding:"required,min=1"`
	EventID int    `json:"event_id" binding:"required,min=1"`
	Amount  string `json:"amount" binding:"required"`
}

func (g *Gateway) CreateBet(c *gin.Context) {
	var httpReq CreateBetHTTPReq
	if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&httpReq); err != nil {
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid request body: " + err.Error()})
		return
	}
	amount, err := decimal.NewFromString(httpReq.Amount)
	if err != nil {
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "invalid amount format, use string like '100.50'"})
		return
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(c.Request.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	bet, status, err := g.BetClient.CreateBet(ctx, httpReq.UserID, httpReq.EventID, amount)
	if err != nil && status == "" {
		code := http.StatusBadGateway
		if errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
			code = http.StatusGatewayTimeout
		}
		c.JSON(code, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
		return
	}

	switch status {
	case "OK":
		c.JSON(http.StatusOK, bet)
		return
	case "NOT_FOUND":
		c.JSON(http.StatusNotFound, gin.H{"error": "event not found"})
		return
	case "EVENT_CLOSED":
		c.JSON(http.StatusConflict, gin.H{"error": "—Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã—Ç–æ –∏–ª–∏ –Ω–µ –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞–µ—Ç —Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫–∏"})
		return
	case "VALIDATION_ERROR":
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
		return
	default: // INTERNAL_ERROR –∏ –ø—Ä–æ—á–∏–µ.
		c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
		return
	}
}

func (g *Gateway) GetBetDetails(c *gin.Context) {
	id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Param("id"))
	if err != nil {
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "bad id"})
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(c.Request.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	details, status, err := g.BetClient.GetBetDetails(ctx, id)
	if err != nil && status == "" {
		code := http.StatusBadGateway
		if errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
			code = http.StatusGatewayTimeout
		}
		c.JSON(code, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
		return
	}

	switch status {
	case "OK":
		c.JSON(http.StatusOK, details)
	case "NOT_FOUND":
		message := "bet or event not found"
		if err != nil {
			message = err.Error()
		}
		c.JSON(http.StatusNotFound, gin.H{"error": message})
	case "VALIDATION_ERROR":
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
	default:
		message := "internal error"
		if err != nil {
			message = err.Error()
		}
		c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": message})
	}
}

func (g *Gateway) GetBetsByEvent(c *gin.Context) {
	eventID, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Param("id"))
	if err != nil || eventID <= 0 {
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": "bad event id"})
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(c.Request.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	result, status, err := g.BetClient.GetBetsByEvent(ctx, eventID)

	if err != nil && status == "" {
		code := http.StatusBadGateway
		if errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
			code = http.StatusGatewayTimeout
		}
		c.JSON(code, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
		return
	}

	switch status {
	case "OK":
		c.JSON(http.StatusOK, result)

	case "VALIDATION_ERROR":
		message := "invalid event id"
		if err != nil {
			message = err.Error()
		}
		c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": message})

	default:
		message := "internal error"
		if err != nil {
			message = err.Error()
		}
		c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": message})
	}
}

    –ó–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã –ø–µ—Ä–µ–¥–∞—é—Ç correlationId –∏ replyTo. replyTo –∑–¥–µ—Å—å —è–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç—Å—è –ø—Ä–∏–∫–ª–∞–¥–Ω—ã–º
    –∑–∞–≥–æ–ª–æ–≤–∫–æ–º —Å –∏–º–µ–Ω–µ–º –æ–¥–Ω–æ–π –∏–∑ –∑–∞—Ä–∞–Ω–µ–µ –æ–ø—Ä–µ–¥–µ–ª—ë–Ω–Ω—ã—Ö –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥–µ–π –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–æ–≤, –∞ –Ω–µ
    –∞–≤—Ç–æ–º–∞—Ç–∏—á–µ—Å–∫–æ–π –Ω–∞—Å—Ç—Ä–æ–π–∫–æ–π –º–∞—Ä—à—Ä—É—Ç–∏–∑–∞—Ü–∏–∏ RabbitMQ.

  messages:

            type: string
            description: –ò–¥–µ–Ω—Ç–∏—Ñ–∏–∫–∞—Ç–æ—Ä –¥–ª—è —Å–æ–ø–æ—Å—Ç–∞–≤–ª–µ–Ω–∏—è –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–∞ –∏ –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∞.

# –°–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å –±—É–∫–º–µ–∫–µ—Ä—Å–∫–æ–π –∫–æ–Ω—Ç–æ—Ä—ã

–ü—Ä–µ–¥–æ—Å—Ç–∞–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç –≤–æ–∑–º–æ–∂–Ω–æ—Å—Ç–∏:

# –ï–¥–∏–Ω—ã–π Dockerfile –¥–ª—è –≤—Å–µ—Ö —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–æ–≤.
# –°–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å –≤—ã–±–∏—Ä–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è build-–∞—Ä–≥—É–º–µ–Ω—Ç–æ–º SERVICE (gateway | bets | events),
# –∏—Ç–æ–≥–æ–≤—ã–π –æ–±—Ä–∞–∑ - —Ü–µ–ª—å—é (target): gateway –∏–ª–∏ service.
#
#   docker build --build-arg SERVICE=bets --target service -t bets .

FROM golang:1.27.2-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app

# –ü—Ä–æ–∫—Å–∏ –º–æ–∂–Ω–æ –ø–µ—Ä–µ–æ–ø—Ä–µ–¥–µ–ª–∏—Ç—å –±–µ–∑ –ø—Ä–∞–≤–∫–∏ —Ñ–∞–π–ª–∞:
#   docker-compose build --build-arg GOPROXY=https://proxy.golang.org,direct
ARG GOPROXY=https://goproxy.io,direct
ENV GOPROXY=${GOPROXY}

COPY . .

RUN go work edit -dropuse=./tests/e2e

ARG SERVICE
RUN test -n "$SERVICE" || (echo "build arg SERVICE is required" >&2; exit 1)

# –°–∫–∞—á–∏–≤–∞–µ–º —Ç–æ–ª—å–∫–æ –º–æ–¥—É–ª–∏, –Ω—É–∂–Ω—ã–µ —ç—Ç–æ–º—É —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å—É, —Å –ø–æ–≤—Ç–æ—Ä–∞–º–∏ –ø—Ä–∏ —Å–±–æ—è—Ö —Å–µ—Ç–∏.
# –ö—ç—à –º–æ–¥—É–ª–µ–π —Å–æ—Ö—Ä–∞–Ω—è–µ—Ç—Å—è –º–µ–∂–¥—É —Å–±–æ—Ä–∫–∞–º–∏ –∏ –º–µ–∂–¥—É —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–∞–º–∏.
RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/go/pkg/mod \
    for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do \
      go list -deps ./services/${SERVICE}/cmd/main.go >/dev/null && exit 0; \
      echo "dependency download failed (attempt $i), retrying..." >&2; \
      sleep 5; \
    done; \
    exit 1

RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/go/pkg/mod \
    --mount=type=cache,target=/root/.cache/go-build \
    go build -o /service ./services/${SERVICE}/cmd/main.go

# gateway: —à–∞–±–ª–æ–Ω—ã –∏ –ø–æ—Ä—Ç
FROM alpine:latest AS gateway
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=builder /service /app/service
COPY services/gateway/templates /app/templates
EXPOSE 8080
ENTRYPOINT ["/app/service"]

# bets, events –∏ –ª—é–±—ã–µ –¥—Ä—É–≥–∏–µ —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å—ã –±–µ–∑ –¥–æ–ø–æ–ª–Ω–∏—Ç–µ–ª—å–Ω—ã—Ö —Ñ–∞–π–ª–æ–≤
# (–ø–æ—Å–ª–µ–¥–Ω—è—è —Å—Ç–∞–¥–∏—è: —Å–æ–±–∏—Ä–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è, –µ—Å–ª–∏ target –Ω–µ —É–∫–∞–∑–∞–Ω)
FROM alpine:latest AS service
WORKDIR /
COPY --from=builder /service /service
ENTRYPOINT ["/service"]
