module bookmaker/shared

go 1.27.2

require github.com/shopspring/decimal v1.5.0
package rmq

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	amqp "github.com/rabbitmq/amqp091-go"
)

// –∏–º—è rpc –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥–∞, –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–π –±—É–¥–µ—Ç –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞—Ç—å—Å—è –Ω–∞ —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä–µ
type RPCMethodName string

type RPCResponseStatus string

const (
	STATUS_OK    RPCResponseStatus = "ok"
	STATUS_ERROR RPCResponseStatus = "error"
)

// —Ç–∏–ø RPCResponse –æ–ø—Ä–µ–¥–µ–ª—è–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç –æ—Ç —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä–∞
// Err - –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞, –ø—Ä–æ–∏–∑–æ—à–µ–¥—à–∞—è –Ω–∞ —Å—Ç–æ—Ä–æ–Ω–µ —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä–∞
type RPCResponse struct {
	Err     string            `json:"error"`
	Status  RPCResponseStatus `json:"status"`
	Payload json.RawMessage   `json:"payload,omitempty"`
}

// –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫ "—Å—ã—Ä–æ–≥–æ" –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–∞: –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞–µ—Ç —Ç–µ–ª–æ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–∞ –∏ –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ—Ç
// —Ç–µ–ª–æ –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∞ (—É–∂–µ —Å–µ—Ä–∏–∞–ª–∏–∑–æ–≤–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–π RPCResponse).
// nil –≤ –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–µ –æ–∑–Ω–∞—á–∞–µ—Ç ¬´—Ä–µ–∑—É–ª—å—Ç–∞—Ç–∞ –Ω–µ—Ç¬ª (–∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç—É —É–π–¥—ë—Ç –ø—É—Å—Ç–æ–π RPCResponse)
// –µ—Å–ª–∏ –≤–µ—Ä–Ω—É–ª–∞—Å—å –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞, –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç—É —É–π–¥—ë—Ç RPCResponse —Å –∑–∞–ø–æ–ª–Ω–µ–Ω–Ω—ã–º Err
type Handler func(raw []byte) ([]byte, error)

const (
	defaultPrefetch = 16
	publishTimeout  = 5 * time.Second
)

var ErrServerClosed = errors.New("rpc server closed")

type RPCServer struct {
	// –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥—å RabbitMQ, –∏–∑ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä–æ–π —á–∏—Ç–∞–µ–º –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã
	RequestsQueue string
	// –º–∞–∫—Å–∏–º—É–º –æ–¥–Ω–æ–≤—Ä–µ–º–µ–Ω–Ω–æ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–∞—Ç—ã–≤–∞–µ–º—ã—Ö –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–æ–≤.
	// –º–µ–Ω—è—Ç—å –Ω—É–∂–Ω–æ –¥–æ –≤—ã–∑–æ–≤–∞ Start.
	Prefetch int

	consumer    *amqp.Channel
	consumerTag string
	producer    *amqp.Channel

	handlers map[RPCMethodName]Handler
	mu       sync.RWMutex // –∑–∞—â–∏—â–∞–µ—Ç handlers

	stateMu   sync.Mutex // –∑–∞—â–∏—â–∞–µ—Ç started –∏ —Å–æ–≥–ª–∞—Å—É–µ—Ç Start —Å Close
	started   bool
	closing   atomic.Bool
	closeOnce sync.Once
	done      chan struct{}  // –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è –≤ Close
	wg        sync.WaitGroup // listener + –≤—ã–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è—é—â–∏–µ—Å—è –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–∏
}

// —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–µ—Ç —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä –∏ –æ–±—ä—è–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥—å –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–æ–≤.
// —Å–æ–µ–¥–∏–Ω–µ–Ω–∏–µ —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä –Ω–µ –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç: –∏–º –≤–ª–∞–¥–µ–µ—Ç —Ç–æ—Ç, –∫—Ç–æ –µ–≥–æ —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–ª.
func NewServer(conn *amqp.Connection, requestsQueue string) (*RPCServer, error) {
	consumer, err := conn.Channel()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("creating consumer channel: %w", err)
	}

	producer, err := conn.Channel()
	if err != nil {
		consumer.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("creating producer channel: %w", err)
	}

	// durable, –Ω–µ exclusive –∏ –Ω–µ auto-delete: –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥—å –ø–µ—Ä–µ–∂–∏–≤–∞–µ—Ç –ø–µ—Ä–µ–∑–∞–ø—É—Å–∫ —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–∞
	_, err = consumer.QueueDeclare(requestsQueue, true, false, false, false, nil)
	if err != nil {
		consumer.Close()
		producer.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("declare queue: %w", err)
	}

	return &RPCServer{
		RequestsQueue: requestsQueue,
		Prefetch:      defaultPrefetch,
		consumer:      consumer,
		consumerTag:   "rpc-server-" + uuid.NewString(),
		producer:      producer,
		handlers:      make(map[RPCMethodName]Handler),
		done:          make(chan struct{}),
	}, nil
}

// –∑–∞–ø—É—Å–∫–∞–µ—Ç –ø—Ä–æ—Å–ª—É—à–∏–≤–∞–Ω–∏–µ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å–æ–≤ –≤ –æ—Ç–¥–µ–ª—å–Ω–æ–π –≥–æ—Ä—É—Ç–∏–Ω–µ
// –∫–∞–∂–¥—ã–π –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–∞—Ç—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è –≤ —Å–≤–æ–µ–π –≥–æ—Ä—É—Ç–∏–Ω–µ, –∏—Ö —á–∏—Å–ª–æ –æ–≥—Ä–∞–Ω–∏—á–µ–Ω–æ Prefetch.
// –æ—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∫–∞: –æ—Ç–º–µ–Ω–∞ ctx –∏–ª–∏ –≤—ã–∑–æ–≤ Close.
// errCh - –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª, –≤ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–π –±—É–¥—É—Ç –∑–∞–ø–∏—Å—ã–≤–∞—Ç—å—Å—è –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∏ –ø—Ä–∏ —Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç–µ –≤–æ—Ä–∫–µ—Ä–∞
func (r *RPCServer) Start(ctx context.Context, errCh chan<- error) error {
	r.stateMu.Lock()
	defer r.stateMu.Unlock()

	if r.closing.Load() {
		return ErrServerClosed
	}
	if r.started {
		return errors.New("rpc server already started")
	}

	if err := r.consumer.Qos(r.Prefetch, 0, false); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("setting qos: %w", err)
	}

	deliveries, err := r.consumer.Consume(
		r.RequestsQueue,
		r.consumerTag,
		false, false, false, false, nil,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("starting consume: %w", err)
	}

	r.started = true

	r.wg.Add(1)
	go r.requestsListener(deliveries, errCh)

	go func() {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			if err := r.Close(); err != nil {
				reportErr(errCh, err)
			}
		case <-r.done:
		}
	}()

	return nil
}

// –≤–æ—Ä–∫–µ—Ä, –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–π –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞–µ—Ç –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã –∏ –∑–∞–ø—É—Å–∫–∞–µ—Ç –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–∏
func (r *RPCServer) requestsListener(deliveries <-chan amqp.Delivery, errCh chan<- error) {
	defer r.wg.Done()

	// —Ü–∏–∫–ª –∑–∞–≤–µ—Ä—à–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è, –∫–æ–≥–¥–∞ consumer –æ—Ç–º–µ–Ω—ë–Ω (Close) –∏–ª–∏ –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã—Ç
	for d := range deliveries {
		r.wg.Add(1) // –±–µ–∑–æ–ø–∞—Å–Ω–æ: listener —Å–∞–º –¥–µ—Ä–∂–∏—Ç —Å—á—ë—Ç—á–∏–∫
		go func(d amqp.Delivery) {
			defer r.wg.Done()
			r.handle(d, errCh)
		}(d)
	}

	// –µ—Å–ª–∏ —ç—Ç–æ –Ω–µ —à—Ç–∞—Ç–Ω—ã–π Close, –∑–Ω–∞—á–∏—Ç –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–ª—Å—è —Å–∞–º (–æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞, –æ–±—Ä—ã–≤)
	if !r.closing.Load() {
		reportErr(errCh, fmt.Errorf("consumer channel closed for queue %s", r.RequestsQueue))
		go r.Close() // –≤ –æ—Ç–¥–µ–ª—å–Ω–æ–π –≥–æ—Ä—É—Ç–∏–Ω–µ: Close –∂–¥—ë—Ç –∑–∞–≤–µ—Ä—à–µ–Ω–∏—è listener'–∞
	}
}

// –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–∞—Ç—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç –æ–¥–∏–Ω –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å: –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫, –æ—Ç–≤–µ—á–∞–µ—Ç (–µ—Å–ª–∏ –Ω—É–∂–Ω–æ), –ø–æ–¥—Ç–≤–µ—Ä–∂–¥–∞–µ—Ç
func (r *RPCServer) handle(d amqp.Delivery, errCh chan<- error) {
	// –ø–æ–¥—Ç–≤–µ—Ä–∂–¥–∞–µ–º –≤ –ª—é–±–æ–º —Å–ª—É—á–∞–µ, —á—Ç–æ–±—ã –Ω–µ –ø–æ–ª—É—á–∏—Ç—å –±–µ—Å–∫–æ–Ω–µ—á–Ω—É—é –ø–æ–≤—Ç–æ—Ä–Ω—É—é
	// –¥–æ—Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫—É "—è–¥–æ–≤–∏—Ç–æ–≥–æ" —Å–æ–æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏—è
	defer func() {
		if err := d.Ack(false); err != nil {
			reportErr(errCh, fmt.Errorf("ack: %w", err))
		}
	}()

	r.mu.RLock()
	handler, ok := r.handlers[RPCMethodName(d.Type)]
	r.mu.RUnlock()

	var (
		res []byte
		err error
	)
	if !ok {
		err = fmt.Errorf("unknown rpc server method %q", d.Type)
	} else {
		res, err = safeCall(handler, d.Body)
	}

	if err != nil {
		reportErr(errCh, fmt.Errorf("method %q: %w", d.Type, err))
	}

	// –µ—Å–ª–∏ ReplyTo –ø—É—Å—Ç–æ, –∑–Ω–∞—á–∏—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç –Ω–µ —Ç—Ä–µ–±—É–µ—Ç—Å—è
	if d.ReplyTo == "" {
		return
	}

	switch {
	case err != nil:
		res = errorResponse(err)
	case res == nil:
		res = emptyResponse()
	}

	r.reply(d, res, errCh)
}

// –ø—É–±–ª–∏–∫—É–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç –≤ –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥—å, —É–∫–∞–∑–∞–Ω–Ω—É—é –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç–æ–º –≤ ReplyTo
func (r *RPCServer) reply(d amqp.Delivery, body []byte, errCh chan<- error) {
	// —Å–≤–æ–π –∫–æ–Ω—Ç–µ–∫—Å—Ç: –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç –Ω–∞ —É–∂–µ –ø—Ä–∏–Ω—è—Ç—ã–π –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å –Ω—É–∂–Ω–æ –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–∏—Ç—å,
	// –¥–∞–∂–µ –µ—Å–ª–∏ ctx —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä–∞ —É–∂–µ –æ—Ç–º–µ–Ω—ë–Ω
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), publishTimeout)
	defer cancel()

	err := r.producer.PublishWithContext(
		ctx,
		"",
		d.ReplyTo,
		false,
		false,
		amqp.Publishing{
			ContentType:   "application/json",
			CorrelationId: d.CorrelationId,
			Body:          body,
		},
	)
	if err != nil {
		reportErr(errCh, fmt.Errorf("publish response: %w", err))
	}
}

// Close –ø–µ—Ä–µ—Å—Ç–∞—ë—Ç –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞—Ç—å –Ω–æ–≤—ã–µ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã, –¥–æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è —É–∂–µ –≤—ã–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è—é—â–∏—Ö—Å—è
// –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–æ–≤ (–æ–Ω–∏ —É—Å–ø–µ–≤–∞—é—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∏—Ç—å –∏ –ø–æ–¥—Ç–≤–µ—Ä–¥–∏—Ç—å —Å–æ–æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏—è) –∏ –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç
// –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª—ã —Å–æ–µ–¥–∏–Ω–µ–Ω–∏–µ –Ω–µ –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è, –ø–æ–≤—Ç–æ—Ä–Ω—ã–µ –≤—ã–∑–æ–≤—ã –±–µ–∑–æ–ø–∞—Å–Ω—ã
// –Ω–µ–ª—å–∑—è –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞—Ç—å –∏–∑ —Å–∞–º–∏—Ö –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–æ–≤: Close –∂–¥—ë—Ç –∏—Ö –∑–∞–≤–µ—Ä—à–µ–Ω–∏—è
func (r *RPCServer) Close() error {
	var err error

	r.closeOnce.Do(func() {
		r.stateMu.Lock()
		r.closing.Store(true)
		started := r.started
		r.stateMu.Unlock()

		close(r.done)

		var cancelErr error
		if started {
			// –Ω–æ–≤—ã–µ –¥–æ—Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫–∏ –ø—Ä–µ–∫—Ä–∞—â–∞—é—Ç—Å—è, —É–∂–µ –ø–æ–ª—É—á–µ–Ω–Ω—ã–µ –¥–æ—á–∏—Ç—ã–≤–∞—é—Ç—Å—è
			cancelErr = r.consumer.Cancel(r.consumerTag, false)
			if cancelErr != nil {
				_ = r.consumer.Close() // –ø—Ä–∏–Ω—É–¥–∏—Ç–µ–ª—å–Ω–æ –∑–∞–∫—Ä—ã–≤–∞–µ–º deliveries
			}
		}

		r.wg.Wait() // listener –∏ –≤—Å–µ –≤—ã–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è—é—â–∏–µ—Å—è –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–∏

		err = errors.Join(cancelErr, r.consumer.Close(), r.producer.Close())
	})

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("closing RPCServer: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// —É—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∏—Ç—å –Ω–æ–≤—ã–π –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫
func (r *RPCServer) HandleRaw(name string, handler Handler) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.handlers[RPCMethodName(name)] = handler
	r.mu.Unlock()
}

// —É—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∏—Ç—å –Ω–æ–≤—ã–π –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫, –Ω–æ —Å –æ–±–µ—Ä—Ç–∫–æ–π
func RegisterHandler[T, B any](r *RPCServer, mname RPCMethodName, handler func(T) (B, RPCResponseStatus, error)) {
	r.HandleRaw(string(mname), HandlerWrap(handler))
}

// —É—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∏—Ç—å –Ω–æ–≤—ã–π –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫ –±–µ–∑ –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ–º–æ–≥–æ –∑–Ω–∞—á–µ–Ω–∏—è
func RegisterHandlerNoRes[T any](r *RPCServer, mname RPCMethodName, handler func(T) error) {
	r.HandleRaw(string(mname), HandlerWrapNoRes(handler))
}

// –æ–±–µ—Ä—Ç–∫–∞ –Ω–∞–¥ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–æ–º, –¥–µ—Å–µ—Ä–∏–∞–ª–∏–∑—É–µ—Ç –∞—Ä–≥—É–º–µ–Ω—Ç—ã –∏–∑ JSON –≤ T,
// –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç —Å–æ–±—Å—Ç–≤–µ–Ω–Ω–æ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫, –ø–æ–º–µ—â–∞–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç –≤ RPCResponse –∏
// —Å–µ—Ä–∏–∞–ª–∏–∑—É–µ—Ç –µ–≥–æ –≤ json.
// –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–∞ –Ω–µ —Ç–µ—Ä—è–µ—Ç—Å—è: –æ–Ω–∞ –ø–æ–ø–∞–¥–∞–µ—Ç –≤ RPCResponse.Err
func HandlerWrap[T, B any](f func(T) (B, RPCResponseStatus, error)) Handler {
	return func(raw []byte) ([]byte, error) {
		var arg T
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &arg); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("unmarshaling json arg: %w", err)
		}

		res, status, err := f(arg)
		if err != nil {
			return json.Marshal(RPCResponse{Err: err.Error(), Status: status})
		}

		rawRes, err := json.Marshal(res)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("marshaling json res: %w", err)
		}

		rawResponse, err := json.Marshal(RPCResponse{Status: status, Payload: rawRes})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("marshaling json response: %w", err)
		}

		return rawResponse, nil
	}
}

// –æ–±–µ—Ä—Ç–∫–∞ –Ω–∞–¥ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫–æ–º, –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–π –Ω–µ –ø—Ä–µ–¥–ø–æ–ª–∞–≥–∞–µ—Ç –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—Ç–∞ –∑–Ω–∞—á–µ–Ω–∏—è,
// –¥–µ—Å–µ—Ä–∏–∞–ª–∏–∑—É–µ—Ç –∞—Ä–≥—É–º–µ–Ω—Ç—ã –∏–∑ JSON –≤ T, –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç —Å–æ–±—Å—Ç–≤–µ–Ω–Ω–æ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫.
// –†–µ–∑—É–ª—å—Ç–∞—Ç –≤—Å–µ–≥–¥–∞ nil; –µ—Å–ª–∏ –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç –≤—Å—ë –∂–µ –∂–¥—ë—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç, –µ–º—É —É–π–¥—ë—Ç –ø—É—Å—Ç–æ–π
// RPCResponse (–∏–ª–∏ RPCResponse —Å –æ—à–∏–±–∫–æ–π, –µ—Å–ª–∏ –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫ –≤–µ—Ä–Ω—É–ª error)
func HandlerWrapNoRes[T any](f func(T) error) Handler {
	return func(raw []byte) ([]byte, error) {
		var arg T
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &arg); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("unmarshaling json arg: %w", err)
		}

		return nil, f(arg)
	}
}

// –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç –æ–±—Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç—á–∏–∫, –ø—Ä–µ–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞—è –ø–∞–Ω–∏–∫—É –≤ –æ—à–∏–±–∫—É
func safeCall(h Handler, raw []byte) (res []byte, err error) {
	defer func() {
		if p := recover(); p != nil {
			err = fmt.Errorf("handler panic: %v", p)
		}
	}()
	return h(raw)
}

// —Å–µ—Ä–∏–∞–ª–∏–∑–æ–≤–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–π RPCResponse —Å –æ—à–∏–±–∫–æ–π
func errorResponse(err error) []byte {
	b, mErr := json.Marshal(RPCResponse{Err: err.Error()})
	if mErr != nil {
		return []byte(`{"error":"internal error"}`)
	}
	return b
}

// —Å–µ—Ä–∏–∞–ª–∏–∑–æ–≤–∞–Ω–Ω—ã–π –ø—É—Å—Ç–æ–π RPCResponse
func emptyResponse() []byte {
	b, _ := json.Marshal(RPCResponse{})
	return b
}
package rmq

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log"
	"time"

	amqp "github.com/rabbitmq/amqp091-go"
)

// –ø–æ–¥–∫–ª—é—á–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è –∫ RabbitMQ —Å –ø–æ–≤—Ç–æ—Ä–Ω—ã–º–∏ –ø–æ–ø—ã—Ç–∫–∞–º–∏
// –æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–Ω–∏–µ –º–µ–∂–¥—É –ø–æ–ø—ã—Ç–∫–∞–º–∏ –ø—Ä–µ—Ä—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è –æ—Ç–º–µ–Ω–æ–π ctx
func DialWithRetry(ctx context.Context, url string, attempts int, delay time.Duration) (*amqp.Connection, error) {
	var lastErr error

	for i := 1; i <= attempts; i++ {
		conn, err := amqp.Dial(url)
		if err == nil {
			log.Println("Successfully connected to RabbitMQ")
			return conn, nil
		}

		lastErr = err
		log.Printf("Failed to connect to RabbitMQ (attempt %d/%d): %v", i, attempts, err)

		if i == attempts {
			break
		}

		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil, ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(delay):
		}
	}

	return nil, fmt.Errorf("could not connect to RabbitMQ after %d attempts: %w", attempts, lastErr)
}
github.com/google/uuid v1.6.0 h1:NIvaJDMOsjHA8n1jAhLSgzrAzy1Hgr+hNrb57e+94F0=

package rmq

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	amqp "github.com/rabbitmq/amqp091-go"
)

// —Ç–∏–ø RPCResult –æ–ø—Ä–µ–¥–µ–ª—è–µ—Ç —Ä–µ–∑—É–ª—å—Ç–∞—Ç –ø–æ–ª—É—á–µ–Ω–∏—è –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∞ –æ—Ç —Å–µ—Ä–≤–µ—Ä–∞
// –æ–Ω —Å–æ–¥–µ—Ä–∂–∏—Ç –æ—à–∏–±–∫—É, –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä–∞—è –ø—Ä–æ–∏–∑–æ—à–ª–∞ –Ω–∞ —Å—Ç–æ—Ä–æ–Ω–µ –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç–∞
type RPCResult struct {
	Response RPCResponse
	Err      error
}

type RPCClient struct {
	conn *amqp.Connection

	ResponsesQueue amqp.Queue // –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥—å RabbitMQ, –≤ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä–æ–π –±—É–¥—É—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—ã –Ω–∞ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã —ç—Ç–æ–≥–æ –º–∏–∫—Ä–æ—Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–∞
	consumer       *amqp.Channel

	producer *amqp.Channel

	methodsServices map[RPCMethodName]string // –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥—ã –∏ –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥–∏ –¥–ª—è –Ω–∏—Ö
	methodsMu       *sync.RWMutex

	// –º–∞–ø–∞ –¥–ª—è –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª–æ–≤ –æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–Ω–∏—è –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∞
	// correlationID - –∫–ª—é—á, –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª —Å –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–æ–º - –∑–Ω–∞—á–µ–Ω–∏–µ
	pending   map[string]chan RPCResult
	stopErr   error       // –Ω–µ nil –ø–æ—Å–ª–µ –æ—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∫–∏, –∑–∞—â–∏—â–µ–Ω–æ pendingMu
	closing   atomic.Bool // –≤—ã—Å—Ç–∞–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç—Å—è –≤ Close
	closeOnce sync.Once
	pendingMu *sync.Mutex
}

func NewClient(conn *amqp.Connection, respQueueName string) (*RPCClient, error) {
	consumer, err := conn.Channel()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("creating channel: %w", err)
	}

	producer, err := conn.Channel()
	if err != nil {
		consumer.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("creating channel: %w", err)
	}

	q, err := consumer.QueueDeclare(respQueueName, false, true, true, false, nil)
	if err != nil {
		consumer.Close()
		producer.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("declare queue: %w", err)
	}

	rpc := &RPCClient{
		conn:            conn,
		consumer:        consumer,
		producer:        producer,
		ResponsesQueue:  q,
		methodsServices: make(map[RPCMethodName]string),
		methodsMu:       &sync.RWMutex{},
		pending:         make(map[string]chan RPCResult),
		pendingMu:       &sync.Mutex{},
	}

	return rpc, nil
}

// –¥–æ–±–∞–≤–∏—Ç—å –Ω–æ–≤—ã–π —É–¥–∞–ª–µ–Ω–Ω—ã–π –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥
func (r *RPCClient) AddMethod(mname RPCMethodName, qname string) {
	r.methodsMu.Lock()
	defer r.methodsMu.Unlock()
	r.methodsServices[mname] = qname
}

// –∑–∞–ø—É—Å–∫ –ø—Ä–æ—Å–ª—É—à–∏–≤–∞–Ω–∏—è –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–æ–≤
// errCh - –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª –≤ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–π –±—É–¥—É—Ç –∑–∞–ø–∏—Å—ã–≤–∞—Ç—å—Å—è –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∏ –ø—Ä–∏ —Ä–∞–±–æ—Ç–µ –≤–æ—Ä–∫–µ—Ä–∞
func (r *RPCClient) Start(ctx context.Context, errCh chan<- error) error {
	deliveries, err := r.consumer.Consume(
		r.ResponsesQueue.Name,
		"", false, false, false, false, nil,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("starting consume: %w", err)
	}

	go r.responseListener(ctx, deliveries, errCh)

	return nil
}

// –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–∏—Ç—å —Å–æ–æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏–µ –±–µ–∑ –æ–∂–∏–¥–∞–Ω–∏—è –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∞ (–æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–∏–ª –∏ –∑–∞–±—ã–ª)
// - r - –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç, —á–µ—Ä–µ–∑ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä–æ–≥–æ –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–ª—è–µ–º
// - mname - –∏–º—è –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ–º–æ–≥–æ –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥–∞
// - payload - –∞—Ä–≥—É–º–µ–Ω—Ç—ã
func Send[T any](ctx context.Context, r *RPCClient, mname RPCMethodName, payload T) error {
	request, err := json.Marshal(payload)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling payload json: %w", err)
	}

	r.methodsMu.RLock()
	queue, ok := r.methodsServices[mname]
	r.methodsMu.RUnlock()
	if !ok {
		return fmt.Errorf("unknown rpc send method %q", mname)
	}

	err = r.producer.PublishWithContext(
		ctx,
		"",
		queue,
		false,
		false,
		amqp.Publishing{
			Type:          string(mname),
			ContentType:   "application/json",
			CorrelationId: uuid.New().String(),
			ReplyTo:       "", // –ø—É—Å—Ç–æ, –ø–æ—Ç–æ–º—É —á—Ç–æ –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç –Ω–µ –ø—Ä–µ–¥–ø–æ–ª–∞–≥–∞–µ—Ç—Å—è
			Body:          request,
		},
	)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("publishing message: %w", err)
	}

	return nil
}

// –≤—ã–∑–æ–≤ —É–¥–∞–ª–µ–Ω–Ω–æ–π —Ñ—É–Ω–∫—Ü–∏–∏
// –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç —Å–æ–æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏–µ –∏ –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ—Ç –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª, –≤ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä–æ–º –ø–æ—è–≤–∏—Ç—Å—è –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç
// - mname - –∏–º—è –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ–º–æ–≥–æ –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥–∞
// - payload - –∞—Ä–≥—É–º–µ–Ω—Ç—ã
// –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ—Ç:
// - –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª, –≤ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä–æ –ø–æ—è–≤–∏—Ç—Å—è –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç
// - correlationId
// - –æ—à–∏–±–∫—É
func (r *RPCClient) call(ctx context.Context, mname RPCMethodName, payload json.RawMessage) (<-chan RPCResult, string, error) {
	corID := uuid.New().String()

	r.methodsMu.RLock()
	queue, ok := r.methodsServices[mname]
	if !ok {
		r.methodsMu.RUnlock()
		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("unknown rpc call method %q", mname)
	}
	r.methodsMu.RUnlock()

	resultCh := make(chan RPCResult, 1)
	r.pendingMu.Lock()
	if r.stopErr != nil {
		err := r.stopErr
		r.pendingMu.Unlock()
		return nil, "", err
	}
	r.pending[corID] = resultCh
	r.pendingMu.Unlock()

	err := r.producer.PublishWithContext(
		ctx,
		"",
		queue,
		false,
		false,
		amqp.Publishing{
			Type:          string(mname),
			ContentType:   "application/json",
			CorrelationId: corID,
			ReplyTo:       r.ResponsesQueue.Name,
			Body:          payload,
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		r.removePending(corID)
		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("publishing message: %w", err)
	}

	return resultCh, corID, nil
}

func (r *RPCClient) removePending(id string) {
	r.pendingMu.Lock()
	delete(r.pending, id)
	r.pendingMu.Unlock()
}

// –≤—ã–∑–æ–≤ —É–¥–∞–ª–µ–Ω–Ω–æ–π —Ñ—É–Ω–∫—Ü–∏–∏
// –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç —Å–æ–æ–±—â–µ–Ω–∏–µ –∏ –∂–¥–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∞
// - r - –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç, —É –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä–æ–≥–æ –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ–º (–æ–±—Ö–æ–¥ —Ç–æ–≥–æ, —á—Ç–æ –≤ Go –Ω–µ–ª—å–∑—è –¥–µ–ª–∞—Ç—å –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥—ã —Å –¥–∂–µ–Ω–µ—Ä–∏–∫–∞–º–∏)
// - mname - –∏–º—è –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞–µ–º–æ–≥–æ –º–µ—Ç–æ–¥–∞
// - payload - –∞—Ä–≥—É–º–µ–Ω—Ç—ã
// –≤—Å–µ–≥–¥–∞ –≤–æ–∑–≤—Ä–∞—â–∞–µ—Ç –Ω–µ–ø—É—Å—Ç–æ–π —Å—Ç–∞—Ç—É—Å
func Call[T, B any](ctx context.Context, r *RPCClient, mname RPCMethodName, payload T) (*B, RPCResponseStatus, error) {
	request, err := json.Marshal(payload)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, STATUS_ERROR, fmt.Errorf("marshaling payload json: %w", err)
	}

	resultCh, corID, err := r.call(ctx, mname, request)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, STATUS_ERROR, fmt.Errorf("publish msg: %w", err)
	}
	defer r.removePending(corID)

	select {
	case result := <-resultCh:
		if result.Err != nil {
			return nil, STATUS_ERROR, fmt.Errorf("error receiving response: %w", result.Err)
		}

		response := result.Response
		if response.Err != "" {
			return nil, response.Status, fmt.Errorf("service error: %s", response.Err)
		}

		var data B
		if len(response.Payload) == 0 {
			return &data, response.Status, nil
		}

		if err := json.Unmarshal(response.Payload, &data); err != nil {
			return nil, response.Status, fmt.Errorf("unmarshaling: %w", err)
		}

		return &data, response.Status, nil

	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, STATUS_ERROR, ctx.Err()
	}
}

// –≤–æ—Ä–∫–µ—Ä, –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–π —á–∏—Ç–∞–µ—Ç –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—ã –æ—Ç –¥—Ä—É–≥–∏—Ö –º–∏–∫—Ä–æ—Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–æ–≤ –∏ –ø–æ–º–µ—â–∞–µ—Ç –∏—Ö –≤ —Å–æ–æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç—Å—Ç–≤—É—é—â–∏–µ –∫–∞–Ω–∞–ª—ã
// –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∏ –∑–∞–ø–∏—Å—ã–≤–∞–µ—Ç –≤ errCh
func (r *RPCClient) responseListener(ctx context.Context, deliveries <-chan amqp.Delivery, errCh chan<- error) {
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			r.Close()
			reportErr(errCh, ctx.Err())
			return
		case d, ok := <-deliveries:
			if !ok {
				if r.closing.Load() {
					r.failAllPending(ErrClientClosed)
					return
				}
				err := fmt.Errorf("consumer channel closed for queue %s", r.ResponsesQueue.Name)
				r.failAllPending(err)
				reportErr(errCh, err)
				r.Close()
				return
			}

			r.pendingMu.Lock()
			ch, exists := r.pending[d.CorrelationId]
			delete(r.pending, d.CorrelationId)
			r.pendingMu.Unlock()

			if exists {
				var res RPCResult
				res.Err = json.Unmarshal(d.Body, &res.Response)
				select {
				case ch <- res:
				default: // –±—É—Ñ–µ—Ä —É–∂–µ –∑–∞–Ω—è—Ç, –¥—É–±–ª–∏–∫–∞—Ç
				}
			}
			d.Ack(false)
		}
	}
}

var ErrClientClosed = errors.New("rpc client closed")

func (r *RPCClient) failAllPending(err error) {
	r.pendingMu.Lock()
	defer r.pendingMu.Unlock()
	if r.stopErr == nil {
		r.stopErr = err
	}
	for id, ch := range r.pending {
		select {
		case ch <- RPCResult{Err: err}:
		default:
		}
		delete(r.pending, id)
	}
}

func (r *RPCClient) Close() error {
	var err error
	r.closeOnce.Do(func() {
		r.closing.Store(true)
		err = errors.Join(r.consumer.Close(), r.producer.Close())
		r.failAllPending(ErrClientClosed)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("closing RPCClient: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func reportErr(errCh chan<- error, err error) {
	select {
	case errCh <- err:
	default:
	}
}

github.com/bdragon300/go-asyncapi/run v0.0.0-20260822140252-d266c6c16c75 h1:GgRWvUgqN3nWEdlyRH1/3vXe9sb5R3ZME4wUHvBl458=
// Package genamqp adapts generated AsyncAPI code to the project's RabbitMQ
// conventions. Generated files remain untouched.
package genamqp

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"bookmaker/proto/amqp"

	"github.com/bdragon300/go-asyncapi/run"
)

const (
	HeaderMessageType   = "messageType"
	HeaderCorrelationID = "correlationId"
	HeaderReplyTo       = "replyTo"

	DefaultHandlerTimeout = 5 * time.Second
)

var ErrUnknownMessageType = errors.New("unknown message type")

// Meta contains the routing and correlation headers of an incoming message.
type Meta struct {
	MessageType   string
	CorrelationID string
	ReplyTo       string
}

func MetaFrom(headers run.Headers) Meta {
	return Meta{
		MessageType:   headerString(headers[HeaderMessageType]),
		CorrelationID: headerString(headers[HeaderCorrelationID]),
		ReplyTo:       headerString(headers[HeaderReplyTo]),
	}
}

func headerString(value any) string {
	switch value := value.(type) {
	case string:
		return value
	case []byte:
		return string(value)
	default:
		return ""
	}
}

// Headers are written using the names and scalar values from the AsyncAPI
// contract, rather than the generated code's PascalCase keys and *string values.
type Headers struct {
	MessageType   string
	CorrelationID string
	ReplyTo       string
}

func (h Headers) table() run.Headers {
	result := run.Headers{
		HeaderMessageType:   h.MessageType,
		HeaderCorrelationID: h.CorrelationID,
	}
	if h.ReplyTo != "" {
		result[HeaderReplyTo] = h.ReplyTo
	}
	return result
}

// Publishing is implemented by generated channel types.
type Publishing interface {
	Queue() string
	Publish(context.Context, ...amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error
}

// Publish uses the generated sealer for the payload, then normalizes headers
// and sets the queue name as routing key for channels bound as RabbitMQ queues.
func Publish(
	ctx context.Context,
	channel Publishing,
	seal func(amqp.EnvelopeWriter) error,
	headers Headers,
) error {
	envelope := amqp.NewEnvelopeOut(nil)
	if err := seal(envelope); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("seal %s: %w", headers.MessageType, err)
	}
	envelope.SetHeaders(headers.table())
	envelope.SetRoutingKey(channel.Queue())
	return channel.Publish(ctx, envelope)
}

type Handler func(context.Context, amqp.EnvelopeReader, Meta) error

type Dispatcher struct {
	OnError        func(error)
	HandlerTimeout time.Duration
	handlers       map[string]Handler
}

func NewDispatcher(onError func(error)) *Dispatcher {
	return &Dispatcher{
		OnError:        onError,
		HandlerTimeout: DefaultHandlerTimeout,
		handlers:       make(map[string]Handler),
	}
}

// Handle registers a handler before Run starts.
func (d *Dispatcher) Handle(messageType string, handler Handler) {
	d.handlers[messageType] = handler
}

// Run consumes a queue through one generated Subscribe method and dispatches
// each message by its messageType header.
func (d *Dispatcher) Run(
	ctx context.Context,
	subscribe func(context.Context, func(amqp.EnvelopeReader)) error,
) error {
	return subscribe(ctx, func(envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) {
		d.Dispatch(ctx, envelope)
	})
}

// Dispatch acknowledges every delivery, including unknown or invalid message
// types, so a poison message cannot be redelivered forever.
func (d *Dispatcher) Dispatch(ctx context.Context, envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader) {
	defer func() {
		if err := envelope.Ack(); err != nil {
			d.report(fmt.Errorf("ack: %w", err))
		}
	}()

	meta := MetaFrom(envelope.Headers())
	handler, ok := d.handlers[meta.MessageType]
	if !ok {
		d.report(fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", ErrUnknownMessageType, meta.MessageType))
		return
	}

	timeout := d.HandlerTimeout
	if timeout <= 0 {
		timeout = DefaultHandlerTimeout
	}
	handlerCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), timeout)
	defer cancel()

	if err := callHandler(handlerCtx, handler, envelope, meta); err != nil {
		d.report(fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", meta.MessageType, err))
	}
}

func (d *Dispatcher) report(err error) {
	if d.OnError != nil {
		d.OnError(err)
	}
}

func callHandler(ctx context.Context, handler Handler, envelope amqp.EnvelopeReader, meta Meta) (err error) {
	defer func() {
		if recovered := recover(); recovered != nil {
			err = fmt.Errorf("handler panic: %v", recovered)
		}
	}()
	return handler(ctx, envelope, meta)
}

package store

import "errors"

type Store[T any] interface {
	Get(id int) (T, error)
	Put(id int, obj T) error
	Delete(id int) error
	FindBy(func(T) bool) ([]T, error)
}

type MapStore[T any] struct {
	data map[int]T
}

func NewMapStore[T any]() MapStore[T] {
	m := make(map[int]T)
	return MapStore[T]{
		m,
	}
}

var ErrNotFound = errors.New("object not found")

func (m MapStore[T]) Get(id int) (T, error) {
	obj, found := m.data[id]
	if !found {
		var zero T
		return zero, ErrNotFound
	}
	return obj, nil
}

func (m MapStore[T]) Put(id int, obj T) error {
	m.data[id] = obj
	return nil
}

func (m MapStore[T]) Delete(id int) error {
	delete(m.data, id)
	return nil
}

func (m MapStore[T]) FindBy(f func(t T) bool) ([]T, error) {
	var objs []T
	for _, v := range m.data {
		if f(v) {
			objs = append(objs, v)
		}
	}
	return objs, nil
}

package e2e

import (
	"bytes"

// –ª–æ–∫–∞–ª—å–Ω—ã–µ –∫–æ–ø–∏–∏ DTO –º–æ–¥—É–ª—å –Ω–µ –∑–∞–≤–∏—Å–∏—Ç –æ—Ç –∫–æ–¥–∞ –ø—Ä–æ–µ–∫—Ç–∞ –∏ –ø—Ä–æ–≤–µ—Ä—è–µ—Ç
// –∏–º–µ–Ω–Ω–æ —Ç–æ, —á—Ç–æ –≤–∏–¥–∏—Ç –≤–Ω–µ—à–Ω–∏–π –∫–ª–∏–µ–Ω—Ç
type createBetRequest struct {
	UserID  int    `json:"user_id"`
	EventID int    `json:"event_id"`
	Amount  string `json:"amount"`
}

type betResponse struct {
	ID      int    `json:"id"`
	EventID int    `json:"event_id"`
	Amount  string `json:"amount"`
	User    string `json:"user"`
}

var httpClient = &http.Client{Timeout: 15 * time.Second}

func postRaw(raw []byte) (int, []byte, error) {
	resp, err := httpClient.Post(baseURL+"/bet", "application/json", bytes.NewReader(raw))
	if err != nil {
		return 0, nil, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	return resp.StatusCode, body, err
}

func postBet(req createBetRequest) (int, []byte, error) {
	raw, err := json.Marshal(req)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, nil, err
	}

	return postRaw(raw)
}

func getBet(id int) (int, []byte, error) {
	resp, err := httpClient.Get(fmt.Sprintf("%s/bet/%d", baseURL, id))
	if err != nil {
		return 0, nil, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	return resp.StatusCode, body, err
}

// –≤ –∑–∞–≥–ª—É—à–∫–µ —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–∞ Events —Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏–µ 1 –µ—â—ë –Ω–µ –ø—Ä–æ–∏–∑–æ—à–ª–æ (—Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫–∏ –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞—é—Ç—Å—è),
// —Å–æ–±—ã—Ç–∏—è 2 –∏ 3 —É–∂–µ –ø—Ä–æ–∏–∑–æ—à–ª–∏ (—Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫–∏ –Ω–µ –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞—é—Ç—Å—è)

func TestCreateBet_EventOpen(t *testing.T) {
	code, body, err := postBet(createBetRequest{UserID: 1, EventID: 1, Amount: "100.50"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("POST /bet: %v", err)
	}
	if code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("status = %d, want 200; body: %s", code, body)
	}

	var created betResponse
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &created); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decoding response %q: %v", body, err)
	}
	if created.ID == 0 || created.EventID != 1 || created.User != "User_1" {
		t.Errorf("created = %+v, want non-zero id, event 1, user User_1", created)
	}
	if amount, err := strconv.ParseFloat(created.Amount, 64); err != nil || amount != 100.5 {
		t.Errorf("amount = %q, want 100.5", created.Amount)
	}

	// —Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫–∞ –¥–µ–π—Å—Ç–≤–∏—Ç–µ–ª—å–Ω–æ —Å–æ—Ö—Ä–∞–Ω–µ–Ω–∞ –≤ Bets: —á–∏—Ç–∞–µ–º –µ—ë –æ–±—Ä–∞—Ç–Ω–æ —á–µ—Ä–µ–∑ gateway
	code, body, err = getBet(created.ID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GET /bet/%d: %v", created.ID, err)
	}
	if code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("GET /bet/%d: status = %d, want 200; body: %s", created.ID, code, body)
	}

	var got betResponse
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decoding response %q: %v", body, err)
	}
	if got != created {
		t.Errorf("GET /bet/%d = %+v, want %+v", created.ID, got, created)
	}
}

func TestCreateBet_EventClosed(t *testing.T) {
	for _, eventID := range []int{2, 3} {
		t.Run(fmt.Sprintf("event_%d", eventID), func(t *testing.T) {
			code, body, err := postBet(createBetRequest{UserID: 1, EventID: eventID, Amount: "50"})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("POST /bet: %v", err)
			}
			if code != http.StatusConflict {
				t.Errorf("status = %d, want 409; body: %s", code, body)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCreateBet_EventNotFound(t *testing.T) {
	code, body, err := postBet(createBetRequest{UserID: 1, EventID: 99, Amount: "10"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("POST /bet: %v", err)
	}

	// –∫–æ–Ω–∫—Ä–µ—Ç–Ω—ã–π –∫–æ–¥ –Ω–µ —Ñ–∏–∫—Å–∏—Ä—É–µ–º (—Å–µ–π—á–∞—Å —ç—Ç–æ –æ—à–∏–±–∫–∞ —à–ª—é–∑–∞, –ª—É—á—à–µ –±—ã–ª–æ –±—ã 404/422),
	// –≤–∞–∂–Ω–æ, —á—Ç–æ —Å—Ç–∞–≤–∫–∞ –Ω–µ —Å–æ–∑–¥–∞–Ω–∞ –∏ –ø—Ä–∏—á–∏–Ω–∞ –ø–æ–Ω—è—Ç–Ω–∞ –∏–∑ –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–∞
	if code == http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("status = 200 for unknown event, want an error; body: %s", body)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(string(body), "not found") {
		t.Errorf("body = %s, want it to mention that the event was not found", body)
	}
}

func TestCreateBet_InvalidRequest(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		body string
	}{
		{"–Ω–µ–∫–æ—Ä—Ä–µ–∫—Ç–Ω–∞—è —Å—É–º–º–∞", `{"user_id":1,"event_id":1,"amount":"abc"}`},
		{"–±–∏—Ç—ã–π JSON", `{`},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			code, body, err := postRaw([]byte(tt.body))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("POST /bet: %v", err)
			}
			if code != http.StatusBadRequest {
				t.Errorf("status = %d, want 400; body: %s", code, body)
			}
		})
	}
}

// –ø–∞—Ä–∞–ª–ª–µ–ª—å–Ω—ã–µ –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å—ã —á–µ—Ä–µ–∑ –≤–µ—Å—å —Å—Ç–µ–∫ –Ω–µ –¥–æ–ª–∂–Ω—ã –ø–æ–ª—É—á–∞—Ç—å –æ–¥–∏–Ω–∞–∫–æ–≤—ã–µ id
func TestCreateBet_ConcurrentIDsUnique(t *testing.T) {
	const n = 10

	var (
		mu  sync.Mutex
		ids = make(map[int]struct{}, n)
		wg  sync.WaitGroup
	)

	for i := range n {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()

			code, body, err := postBet(createBetRequest{UserID: i + 1, EventID: 1, Amount: "10"})
			if err != nil {
				t.Errorf("POST /bet: %v", err)
				return
			}
			if code != http.StatusOK {
				t.Errorf("status = %d, want 200; body: %s", code, body)
				return
			}

			var created betResponse
			if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &created); err != nil {
				t.Errorf("decoding response %q: %v", body, err)
				return
			}

			mu.Lock()
			ids[created.ID] = struct{}{}
			mu.Unlock()
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()

	if len(ids) != n {
		t.Errorf("got %d unique bet ids, want %d", len(ids), n)
	}
}

import (

	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log"

	"time"

	"github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-go/modules/compose"

)

const (
	// compose-—Ñ–∞–π–ª –æ—Å–Ω–æ–≤–Ω–æ–≥–æ –ø—Ä–æ–µ–∫—Ç–∞: —Ç–µ—Å—Ç –ø–æ–¥–Ω–∏–º–∞–µ—Ç —Ç–æ—Ç –∂–µ —Å—Ç–µ–∫, —á—Ç–æ –∏ docker compose up
	composeFile = "../../docker-compose.yml"
	projectName = "bookmaker-e2e"
)

// baseURL - –∞–¥—Ä–µ—Å gateway –Ω–∞ —Ö–æ—Å—Ç–µ, –∑–∞–ø–æ–ª–Ω—è–µ—Ç—Å—è –≤ TestMain
var baseURL string

func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
	flag.Parse()
	if testing.Short() {
		fmt.Println("e2e: –ø—Ä–æ–ø—É—â–µ–Ω–æ –≤ —Ä–µ–∂–∏–º–µ -short")
		os.Exit(0)
	}

	os.Exit(run(m))
}

func run(m *testing.M) int {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 15*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()

	// –ø—Ä–∏–Ω—É–¥–∏—Ç–µ–ª—å–Ω–∞—è –æ—á–∏—Å—Ç–∫–∞ –ø–µ—Ä–µ–¥ –∑–∞–ø—É—Å–∫–æ–º –Ω–∞ —Å–ª—É—á–∞–π –∞–≤–∞—Ä–∏–π–Ω–æ–≥–æ –∑–∞–≤–µ—Ä—à–µ–Ω–∏—è –ø—Ä–æ—à–ª–æ–≥–æ —Ç–µ—Å—Ç–∞
	exec.CommandContext(ctx, "docker", "compose", "-f", composeFile, "-p", projectName, "down", "-v", "--remove-orphans").Run()

	if err := dockerComposeBuild(ctx); err != nil {
		log.Printf("e2e: %v", err)
		return 1
	}

	stack, err := compose.NewDockerComposeWith(
		compose.StackIdentifier(projectName),
		compose.WithStackFiles(composeFile),
	)
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("e2e: creating compose stack: %v", err)
		return 1
	}

	defer func() {
		// –æ—Ç–¥–µ–ª—å–Ω—ã–π –∫–æ–Ω—Ç–µ–∫—Å—Ç: ctx –∫ —ç—Ç–æ–º—É –º–æ–º–µ–Ω—Ç—É –º–æ–∂–µ—Ç –±—ã—Ç—å —É–∂–µ –æ—Ç–º–µ–Ω—ë–Ω
		if err := stack.Down(context.Background(), compose.RemoveOrphans(true), compose.RemoveVolumes(true)); err != nil {
			log.Printf("e2e: compose down: %v", err)
		}
	}()

	// –ø–æ—Å–ª–µ –æ—Å—Ç–∞–Ω–æ–≤–∫–∏ —Å—Ç–µ–∫–∞ –ª–æ–≥–∏ –ø—Ä–æ–ø–∞–¥—É—Ç, –ø–æ—ç—Ç–æ–º—É –ø–µ—á–∞—Ç–∞–µ–º –∏—Ö –ø—Ä–∏ –Ω–µ—É–¥–∞—á–µ
	dumpLogs := func() {
		for _, svc := range []string{"gateway", "bets", "events"} {
			c, err := stack.ServiceContainer(context.Background(), svc)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			r, err := c.Logs(context.Background())
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\n===== logs: %s =====\n", svc)
			_, _ = io.Copy(os.Stderr, r)
			_ = r.Close()
		}
	}

	// –∂–¥—ë–º –Ω–µ –ø—Ä–æ—Å—Ç–æ –∑–∞–ø—É—Å–∫–∞ –∫–æ–Ω—Ç–µ–π–Ω–µ—Ä–æ–≤, –∞ –≥–æ—Ç–æ–≤–Ω–æ—Å—Ç–∏ –ø—Ä–∏–ª–æ–∂–µ–Ω–∏–π: –∏–Ω–∞—á–µ –ø–µ—Ä–≤—ã–π
	// –∑–∞–ø—Ä–æ—Å –º–æ–∂–µ—Ç —É–π—Ç–∏ —Ä–∞–Ω—å—à–µ, —á–µ–º —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å—ã –æ–±—ä—è–≤—è—Ç –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥–∏
	err = stack.
		WaitForService("events", wait.ForLog("Events service is running")).
		WaitForService("bets", wait.ForLog("Bets service is running")).
		WaitForService("gateway", wait.ForListeningPort("8080/tcp")).
		Up(ctx, compose.Wait(true))
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("e2e: compose up: %v", err)
		dumpLogs()
		return 1
	}

	gateway, err := stack.ServiceContainer(ctx, "gateway")
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("e2e: getting gateway container: %v", err)
		return 1
	}
	baseURL, err = gateway.PortEndpoint(ctx, "8080/tcp", "http")
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("e2e: getting gateway endpoint: %v", err)
		return 1
	}

	code := m.Run()
	if code != 0 {
		dumpLogs()
	}

	return code
}

// —Å–æ–±–∏—Ä–∞–µ—Ç –æ–±—Ä–∞–∑—ã —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å–æ–≤ –∏–∑ compose-—Ñ–∞–π–ª–∞; –∏–º—è –ø—Ä–æ–µ–∫—Ç–∞ —Å–æ–≤–ø–∞–¥–∞–µ—Ç —Å —Ç–µ–º,
// –ø–æ–¥ –∫–æ—Ç–æ—Ä—ã–º —Å—Ç–µ–∫ –ø–æ–¥–Ω–∏–º–∞–µ—Ç –º–æ–¥—É–ª—å compose
func dockerComposeBuild(ctx context.Context) error {
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "docker", "compose", "-f", composeFile, "-p", projectName, "build")
	cmd.Stdout = os.Stderr
	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr

	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("docker compose build: %w", err)
	}

	return nil
}

	Producer() amqp.Producer
	Consumer() amqp.Consumer
}

func OpenEventsRequestsAMQP(
	ctx context.Context,
	server EventsRequestsServerAMQP,

	opBindings *amqp.OperationBindings,
	security run.AnySecurityScheme,
) (*EventsRequestsAMQP, error) {
	var err error
	chBindings := EventsRequestsBindings{}.AMQP()
	address, err := EventsRequestsAddress().Expand()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var publisher amqp.Publisher
	producer := server.Producer()
	if producer != nil {
		publisher, err = producer.Publisher(
			ctx,
			address,
			&chBindings,
			opBindings,
			security,
		)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	var subscriber amqp.Subscriber
	consumer := server.Consumer()
	if consumer != nil {
		subscriber, err = consumer.Subscriber(
			ctx,
			address,
			&chBindings,
			opBindings,
			security,
		)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}

	return NewEventsRequestsAMQP(

		publisher,
		subscriber,
	), nil
}

	address    run.ParamString
	publisher  amqp.Publisher
	subscriber amqp.Subscriber
	exchange   string
	queue      string
	routingKey string
}

	return c.exchange

	return EventsRequestsBindings{}.AMQP()

func (c EventsRequestsAMQP) Close() (err error) {
	if c.publisher != nil {
		err = errors.Join(err, c.publisher.Close())
	}
	if c.subscriber != nil {
		err = errors.Join(err, c.subscriber.Close())
	}
	return
}

package amqp

import (
	"github.com/bdragon300/go-asyncapi/run"
)

import (
	"io"

	"github.com/rabbitmq/amqp091-go"
)

func NewEnvelopeOut(buf []byte) *EnvelopeOut {
	return &EnvelopeOut{
		Publishing: &amqp091.Publishing{Body: buf},
	}
}

type EnvelopeOut struct {
	*amqp091.Publishing
	routingKey string
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) Write(p []byte) (n int, err error) {
	e.Body = append(e.Body, p...)
	return len(p), nil
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) ResetPayload() {
	e.Body = e.Body[:0]
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) SetHeaders(headers run.Headers) {
	e.Publishing.Headers = map[string]any(headers)
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) SetContentType(contentType string) {
	e.ContentType = contentType
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) SetBindings(bindings MessageBindings) {
	e.Publishing.ContentEncoding = bindings.ContentEncoding
	e.Type = bindings.MessageType
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) SetRoutingKey(routingKey string) {
	e.routingKey = routingKey
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) AsAMQP091Record() *amqp091.Publishing {
	return e.Publishing
}

func (e *EnvelopeOut) RoutingKey() string {
	return e.routingKey
}

func NewEnvelopeIn(delivery *amqp091.Delivery, rd io.Reader) *EnvelopeIn {
	return &EnvelopeIn{
		Delivery: delivery,
		reader:   rd,
	}
}

type EnvelopeIn struct {
	*amqp091.Delivery
	reader io.Reader
}

func (e EnvelopeIn) Read(p []byte) (n int, err error) {
	return e.reader.Read(p)
}

func (e EnvelopeIn) Headers() run.Headers {
	return map[string]any(e.Delivery.Headers)
}

func (e EnvelopeIn) Ack() error {
	return e.Delivery.Ack(false)
}

func (e EnvelopeIn) Nack(requeue bool) error {
	return e.Delivery.Nack(false, requeue)
}
// Code generated by go-asyncapi tool. DO NOT EDIT.

package amqp

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/rabbitmq/amqp091-go"
)

type PublishChannel struct {
	*amqp091.Channel
	exchangeName      string
	channelBindings   *ChannelBindings
	operationBindings *OperationBindings
}

type ImplementationRecord interface {
	AsAMQP091Record() *amqp091.Publishing
	RoutingKey() string
}

func (p PublishChannel) Send(ctx context.Context, envelopes ...EnvelopeWriter) error {
	var err error
	for _, envelope := range envelopes {
		rm := envelope.(ImplementationRecord)
		record := rm.AsAMQP091Record()
		record.Timestamp = time.Time{}
		var mandatory bool
		if p.operationBindings != nil {
			record.DeliveryMode = uint8(p.operationBindings.DeliveryMode)
			record.Priority = uint8(p.operationBindings.Priority)
			if p.operationBindings.Timestamp {
				record.Timestamp = time.Now()
			}
			record.ReplyTo = p.operationBindings.ReplyTo
			record.UserId = p.operationBindings.UserID
			if p.operationBindings.Expiration > 0 {
				record.Expiration = p.operationBindings.Expiration.String()
			}
			if len(p.operationBindings.CC) > 0 {
				record.Headers["CC"] = p.operationBindings.CC
			}
			if len(p.operationBindings.BCC) > 0 {
				record.Headers["BCC"] = p.operationBindings.BCC
			}
			mandatory = p.operationBindings.Mandatory
		}

		err = errors.Join(err, p.Channel.PublishWithContext(
			ctx, p.exchangeName, rm.RoutingKey(), mandatory, false, *record,
		))
	}
	return err
}

package amqp

import (
	"github.com/bdragon300/go-asyncapi/run"
)

import (
	"context"
	"io"
)

// Pub
type (
	Producer interface {
		Publisher(ctx context.Context, address string, chBindings *ChannelBindings, opBindings *OperationBindings, security run.AnySecurityScheme) (Publisher, error)
	}
	Publisher interface {
		Send(ctx context.Context, envelopes ...EnvelopeWriter) error
		Close() error
	}
	EnvelopeWriter interface {
		io.Writer
		ResetPayload()
		SetHeaders(headers run.Headers)
		SetContentType(contentType string)
		SetBindings(bindings MessageBindings)

		SetRoutingKey(tag string) // TODO: remove? sets in SealEnvelope
	}
)

type EnvelopeMarshaler interface {
	MarshalEnvelopeAMQP(envelope EnvelopeWriter) error
}

// Sub
type (
	Consumer interface {
		Subscriber(ctx context.Context, address string, chBindings *ChannelBindings, opBindings *OperationBindings, security run.AnySecurityScheme) (Subscriber, error)
	}
	Subscriber interface {
		Receive(ctx context.Context, cb func(envelope EnvelopeReader)) error
		Close() error
	}
	EnvelopeReader interface {
		io.Reader
		Headers() run.Headers

		Ack() error
		Nack(requeue bool) error
		Reject(requeue bool) error
	}
)

type EnvelopeUnmarshaler interface {
	UnmarshalEnvelopeAMQP(envelope EnvelopeReader) error
}

package amqp

import (
	"github.com/bdragon300/go-asyncapi/run"
)

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/rabbitmq/amqp091-go"
)

type SubscribeChannel struct {
	*amqp091.Channel
	// ConsumerTag uniquely identifies the consumer process. If empty, a unique tag is generated.
	ConsumerTag string
	// Additional arguments for the consumer. See ConsumeWithContext docs for details.
	ConsumeArgs amqp091.Table

	queueName         string
	channelBindings   *ChannelBindings
	operationBindings *OperationBindings
}

func (s SubscribeChannel) Receive(ctx context.Context, cb func(envelope EnvelopeReader)) (err error) {
	// TODO: consumer tag in x- schema argument
	// Separate context is used to stop consumer process for a particular consumer tag on function exit.
	consumerCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	defer cancel()

	var ack, exclusive bool
	if s.operationBindings != nil {
		ack = s.operationBindings.Ack
	}
	if s.channelBindings != nil {
		exclusive = run.FromPtrOrZero(s.channelBindings.QueueConfiguration.Exclusive)
	}
	deliveries, err := s.ConsumeWithContext(
		consumerCtx,
		s.queueName,
		s.ConsumerTag,
		ack,
		exclusive,
		false,
		false,
		s.ConsumeArgs,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	for delivery := range deliveries {
		evlp := NewEnvelopeIn(&delivery, bytes.NewReader(delivery.Body))
		cb(evlp)
		if ack {
			if e := s.Ack(delivery.DeliveryTag, false); e != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("ack: %w", e)
			}
		}
	}
	return
}
// Code generated by go-asyncapi tool. DO NOT EDIT.

package amqp

import (
	"github.com/bdragon300/go-asyncapi/run"
)

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/rabbitmq/amqp091-go"
)

func NewClient(serverURL string, bindings *ServerBindings, security run.AnySecurityScheme) (*Client, error) {
	var conn *amqp091.Connection
	var err error

	switch s := security.(type) {
	case run.UserPasswordSecurity:
		user, pass := s.UserPassword()
		amqpAuth := &amqp091.PlainAuth{
			Username: user,
			Password: pass,
		}
		conn, err = amqp091.DialConfig(serverURL, amqp091.Config{
			SASL: []amqp091.Authentication{amqpAuth},
		})
	case nil:
		conn, err = amqp091.Dial(serverURL)
	default:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unsupported security scheme %T", security.AuthType())
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &Client{
		Connection: conn,
		bindings:   bindings,
	}, nil
}

type Client struct {
	*amqp091.Connection
	bindings *ServerBindings
}

func (c Client) Publisher(_ context.Context, _ string, chb *ChannelBindings, opb *OperationBindings, security run.AnySecurityScheme) (Publisher, error) {
	if security != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("security schemes for publishers are not supported")
	}
	ch, err := c.Channel()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	var exchangeName string // By default, publish to the default exchange with empty name
	if chb != nil {
		ec := chb.ExchangeConfiguration
		if ec.Name != nil {
			exchangeName = *ec.Name
		}
		declare := ec.Type != "" || ec.Durable != nil || ec.AutoDelete != nil || ec.VHost != ""
		if declare {
			err = ch.ExchangeDeclare(
				exchangeName,
				string(ec.Type),
				run.FromPtrOrZero(ec.Durable),
				run.FromPtrOrZero(ec.AutoDelete),
				false,
				false,
				nil,
			)
			if err != nil {
				err = errors.Join(err, ch.Close())
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("exchange declare: %w", err)
			}
		}
	}
	return &PublishChannel{
		Channel:           ch,
		exchangeName:      exchangeName,
		channelBindings:   chb,
		operationBindings: opb,
	}, nil
}

func (c Client) Subscriber(_ context.Context, address string, chb *ChannelBindings, opb *OperationBindings, security run.AnySecurityScheme) (Subscriber, error) {
	if security != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("security schemes for subscribers are not supported")
	}
	ch, err := c.Channel()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	// queueName==channelBindings.QueueConfiguration.Name or address
	// exchangeName==channelBindings.ExchangeConfiguration.Name or empty (i.e. default AMQP exchange)
	// If queue.is=="routingKey" (default), then routingKey=address
	// If queue.is=="queue", then routingKey="#"
	exchangeName := amqp091.DefaultExchange
	routingKey := address
	queueName := address
	var durable, autoDelete, exclusive bool
	if chb != nil {
		if chb.Is == ChannelTypeQueue {
			routingKey = "#" // Receive all messages
		}
		qc := chb.QueueConfiguration
		if qc.Name != "" {
			queueName = qc.Name
		}
		durable, autoDelete, exclusive = run.FromPtrOrZero(qc.Durable), run.FromPtrOrZero(qc.AutoDelete), run.FromPtrOrZero(qc.Exclusive)
		exchangeName = run.FromPtrOrZero(chb.ExchangeConfiguration.Name)
	}
	if exchangeName == amqp091.DefaultExchange {
		_, err = ch.QueueDeclare(queueName, durable, autoDelete, exclusive, false, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, errors.Join(fmt.Errorf("queue declare: %w", err), ch.Close())
		}
	} else {
		// TODO: binding key in x- schema argument
		if err = ch.QueueBind(queueName, routingKey, exchangeName, false, nil); err != nil {
			return nil, errors.Join(fmt.Errorf("queue bind: %w", err), ch.Close())
		}
	}

	return &SubscribeChannel{
		Channel:           ch,
		queueName:         queueName,
		channelBindings:   chb,
		operationBindings: opb,
	}, nil
}

package amqp

import (
	"time"
)

type DeliveryMode int

const (
	DeliveryModeTransient  DeliveryMode = 1
	DeliveryModePersistent DeliveryMode = 2
)

type ExchangeType string

const (
	ExchangeTypeDefault ExchangeType = "default"
	ExchangeTypeTopic   ExchangeType = "topic"
	ExchangeTypeDirect  ExchangeType = "direct"
	ExchangeTypeFanout  ExchangeType = "fanout"
	ExchangeTypeHeaders ExchangeType = "headers"
)

type ChannelType string

const (
	ChannelTypeRoutingKey ChannelType = "routingKey"
	ChannelTypeQueue      ChannelType = "queue"
)

type (
	ServerBindings struct{}

	ChannelBindings struct {
		Is                    ChannelType
		ExchangeConfiguration ExchangeConfiguration
		QueueConfiguration    QueueConfiguration
	}

	ExchangeConfiguration struct {
		Name       *string // Empty name points to default broker exchange
		Type       ExchangeType
		Durable    *bool
		AutoDelete *bool
		VHost      string
	}

	QueueConfiguration struct {
		Name       string
		Durable    *bool
		Exclusive  *bool
		AutoDelete *bool
		VHost      string
	}

	OperationBindings struct {
		Expiration   time.Duration
		UserID       string
		CC           []string
		Priority     int
		DeliveryMode DeliveryMode
		Mandatory    bool
		BCC          []string
		ReplyTo      string // TODO: remove, asyncapi 3.0.0 only
		Timestamp    bool
		Ack          bool
	}

	MessageBindings struct {
		ContentEncoding string
		MessageType     string
	}
)

package messages

import (
	"bookmaker/proto/amqp"
	"bookmaker/schemas"

	"github.com/bdragon300/go-asyncapi/run"
	"io"
)

type GetEventRequestSender interface {
	SetPayload(payload schemas.GetEventRequest) *GetEventRequestOut
	SetHeaders(headers struct {
		MessageType   *string `json:"messageType"`
		CorrelationID *string `json:"correlationId"`
		// ReplyTo--–ü—Ä–∏–∫–ª–∞–¥–Ω–æ–π –∞–¥—Ä–µ—Å –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥–∏ –æ—Ç–≤–µ—Ç–æ–≤; –æ—Ç–ø—Ä–∞–≤–∏—Ç–µ–ª—å –≤—ã–±–∏—Ä–∞–µ—Ç –µ–≥–æ –ø–æ –≤—ã–∑—ã–≤–∞—é—â–µ–º—É —Å–µ—Ä–≤–∏—Å—É.
		ReplyTo *string `json:"replyTo"`
	}) *GetEventRequestOut
	SetCorrelationID(value *string) *GetEventRequestOut
}

type GetEventRequestOut struct {
	Payload schemas.GetEventRequest
	Headers struct {

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return &GatewayCreateBetAMQP{
		Channel: ch,
	}, nil
}

	Close() error

	SealCreateBetRequest(amqp.EnvelopeWriter, channels.BetsRequestsEnvelopeMarshalerAMQP) error
	PublishCreateBetRequest(context.Context, channels.BetsRequestsEnvelopeMarshalerAMQP) error

	UnsealCreateBetRequest(amqp.EnvelopeReader, channels.BetsRequestsEnvelopeUnmarshalerAMQP) error
	SubscribeCreateBetRequest(context.Context, func(messages.CreateBetRequestReceiver)) error
}

type GatewayCreateBetAMQP struct {
	Channel GatewayCreateBetChannelAMQP
}

func (c GatewayCreateBetAMQP) Close() error {
	return c.Channel.Close()
}

	envelope amqp.EnvelopeWriter,
	message channels.BetsRequestsEnvelopeMarshalerAMQP,
) error {
	return o.Channel.SealCreateBetRequest(envelope, message)

	ctx context.Context,

func RabbitmqURL() (*url.URL, error) {
	return &url.URL{Scheme: "amqp", Host: "localhost:5672", Path: ""}, nil
}

func NewRabbitmq(producer amqp.Producer, consumer amqp.Consumer) *Rabbitmq {
	return &Rabbitmq{
		producer: producer,
		consumer: consumer,
	}
}

type RabbitmqClosable struct {
	Rabbitmq
}

func (c RabbitmqClosable) Close() error {
	var err error
	if v, ok := any(c.producer).(io.Closer); ok {
		err = errors.Join(err, v.Close())
	}
	if v, ok := any(c.consumer).(io.Closer); ok {
		err = errors.Join(err, v.Close())
	}
	return err
}

func ConnectRabbitmqBidi(
	ctx context.Context,
	url *url.URL,

) (*RabbitmqClosable, error) {
	var bindings *amqp.ServerBindings
	client, err := amqp.NewClient(url.String(), bindings, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	producer, consumer := client, client
	return &RabbitmqClosable{
		Rabbitmq{producer: producer, consumer: consumer},
	}, nil
}

services:
  rabbitmq:
    image: rabbitmq:3.13-management
    container_name: rabbitmq
    ports:
      - "5672:5672"
      - "15672:15672"
    healthcheck:
      # –ø—Ä–æ–≤–µ—Ä—è–µ—Ç, —á—Ç–æ –ø–æ—Ä—Ç—ã —Ä–µ–∞–ª—å–Ω–æ –ø—Ä–∏–Ω–∏–º–∞—é—Ç —Å–æ–µ–¥–∏–Ω–µ–Ω–∏—è
      # (ping –ø—Ä–æ–≤–µ—Ä—è–µ—Ç —Ç–æ–ª—å–∫–æ, —á—Ç–æ Erlang-–Ω–æ–¥–∞ –∂–∏–≤–∞)
      test: ["CMD", "rabbitmq-diagnostics", "-q", "check_port_connectivity"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 10
      start_period: 15s
    networks:
      - app-network

  gateway:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
      target: gateway
      args:
        SERVICE: gateway
    container_name: gateway
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    environment:
      - BROKER_URL=amqp://guest:guest@rabbitmq:5672/
    depends_on:
      rabbitmq:
        condition: service_healthy
    restart: on-failure
    networks:
      - app-network

  bets:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
      target: service
      args:
        SERVICE: bets
    container_name: bets
    environment:
      - BROKER_URL=amqp://guest:guest@rabbitmq:5672/
    depends_on:
      rabbitmq:
        condition: service_healthy
      events:
        condition: service_started # events –æ–±—ä—è–≤–ª—è–µ—Ç –æ—á–µ—Ä–µ–¥—å events.requests
    restart: on-failure
    networks:
      - app-network

  events:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
      target: service
      args:
        SERVICE: events
    container_name: events
    environment:
      - BROKER_URL=amqp://guest:guest@rabbitmq:5672/
    depends_on:
      rabbitmq:
        condition: service_healthy
    restart: on-failure
    networks:
      - app-network

networks:
  app-network:
    driver: bridge

# Lines that start with '#' are comments.
# For a project mostly in C, the following would be a good set of
# exclude patterns (uncomment them if you want to use them):
# *.[oa]
# *~
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed.
# Called by "git commit" with no arguments.  The hook should
# exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate message if
# it wants to stop the commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-commit".

if git rev-parse --verify HEAD >/dev/null 2>&1
then
	against=HEAD
else
	# Initial commit: diff against an empty tree object
	against=$(git hash-object -t tree /dev/null)
fi

# If you want to allow non-ASCII filenames set this variable to true.
allownonascii=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allownonascii)

# Redirect output to stderr.
exec 1>&2

# Cross platform projects tend to avoid non-ASCII filenames; prevent
# them from being added to the repository. We exploit the fact that the
# printable range starts at the space character and ends with tilde.
if [ "$allownonascii" != "true" ] &&
	# Note that the use of brackets around a tr range is ok here, (it's
	# even required, for portability to Solaris 10's /usr/bin/tr), since
	# the square bracket bytes happen to fall in the designated range.
	test $(git diff --cached --name-only --diff-filter=A -z $against |
	  LC_ALL=C tr -d '[ -~]\0' | wc -c) != 0
then
	cat <<\EOF
Error: Attempt to add a non-ASCII file name.

This can cause problems if you want to work with people on other platforms.

To be portable it is advisable to rename the file.

If you know what you are doing you can disable this check using:

  git config hooks.allownonascii true
EOF
	exit 1
fi

# If there are whitespace errors, print the offending file names and fail.
exec git diff-index --check --cached $against --
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to prepare the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with the name of the file that has the
# commit message, followed by the description of the commit
# message's source.  The hook's purpose is to edit the commit
# message file.  If the hook fails with a non-zero status,
# the commit is aborted.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "prepare-commit-msg".

# This hook includes three examples. The first one removes the
# "# Please enter the commit message..." help message.
#
# The second includes the output of "git diff --name-status -r"
# into the message, just before the "git status" output.  It is
# commented because it doesn't cope with --amend or with squashed
# commits.
#
# The third example adds a Signed-off-by line to the message, that can
# still be edited.  This is rarely a good idea.

COMMIT_MSG_FILE=$1
COMMIT_SOURCE=$2
SHA1=$3

/usr/bin/perl -i.bak -ne 'print unless(m/^. Please enter the commit message/..m/^#$/)' "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"

# case "$COMMIT_SOURCE,$SHA1" in
#  ,|template,)
#    /usr/bin/perl -i.bak -pe '
#       print "\n" . `git diff --cached --name-status -r`
# 	 if /^#/ && $first++ == 0' "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE" ;;
#  *) ;;
# esac

# SOB=$(git var GIT_COMMITTER_IDENT | sed -n 's/^\(.*>\).*$/Signed-off-by: \1/p')
# git interpret-trailers --in-place --trailer "$SOB" "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"
# if test -z "$COMMIT_SOURCE"
# then
#   /usr/bin/perl -i.bak -pe 'print "\n" if !$first_line++' "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"
# fi
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed
# by applypatch from an e-mail message.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an
# appropriate message if it wants to stop the commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-applypatch".

. git-sh-setup
precommit="$(git rev-parse --git-path hooks/pre-commit)"
test -x "$precommit" && exec "$precommit" ${1+"$@"}
:
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to make use of push options.
# The example simply echoes all push options that start with 'echoback='
# and rejects all pushes when the "reject" push option is used.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-receive".

if test -n "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
then
	i=0
	while test "$i" -lt "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
	do
		eval "value=\$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_$i"
		case "$value" in
		echoback=*)
			echo "echo from the pre-receive-hook: ${value#*=}" >&2
			;;
		reject)
			exit 1
		esac
		i=$((i + 1))
	done
fi
#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to validate a patch (and/or patch series) before
# sending it via email.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate
# message if it wants to prevent the email(s) from being sent.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "sendemail-validate".
#
# By default, it will only check that the patch(es) can be applied on top of
# the default upstream branch without conflicts in a secondary worktree. After
# validation (successful or not) of the last patch of a series, the worktree
# will be deleted.
#
# The following config variables can be set to change the default remote and
# remote ref that are used to apply the patches against:
#
#   sendemail.validateRemote (default: origin)
#   sendemail.validateRemoteRef (default: HEAD)
#
# Replace the TODO placeholders with appropriate checks according to your
# needs.

validate_cover_letter () {
	file="$1"
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks (e.g. spell checking).
	true
}

validate_patch () {
	file="$1"
	# Ensure that the patch applies without conflicts.
	git am -3 "$file" || return
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for this patch
	# (e.g. checkpatch.pl).
	true
}

validate_series () {
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for the whole series
	# (e.g. quick build, coding style checks, etc.).
	true
}

# main -------------------------------------------------------------------------

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = 1
then
	remote=$(git config --default origin --get sendemail.validateRemote) &&
	ref=$(git config --default HEAD --get sendemail.validateRemoteRef) &&
	worktree=$(mktemp --tmpdir -d sendemail-validate.XXXXXXX) &&
	git worktree add -fd --checkout "$worktree" "refs/remotes/$remote/$ref" &&
	git config --replace-all sendemail.validateWorktree "$worktree"
else
	worktree=$(git config --get sendemail.validateWorktree)
fi || {
	echo "sendemail-validate: error: failed to prepare worktree" >&2
	exit 1
}

unset GIT_DIR GIT_WORK_TREE
cd "$worktree" &&

if grep -q "^diff --git " "$1"
then
	validate_patch "$1"
else
	validate_cover_letter "$1"
fi &&

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_TOTAL"
then
	git config --unset-all sendemail.validateWorktree &&
	trap 'git worktree remove -ff "$worktree"' EXIT &&
	validate_series
fi
#!/usr/bin/perl

use strict;
use warnings;
use IPC::Open2;

# An example hook script to integrate Watchman
# (https://facebook.github.io/watchman/) with git to speed up detecting
# new and modified files.
#
# The hook is passed a version (currently 2) and last update token
# formatted as a string and outputs to stdout a new update token and
# all files that have been modified since the update token. Paths must
# be relative to the root of the working tree and separated by a single NUL.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "query-watchman" and set
# 'git config core.fsmonitor .git/hooks/query-watchman'
#
my ($version, $last_update_token) = @ARGV;

# Uncomment for debugging
# print STDERR "$0 $version $last_update_token\n";

# Check the hook interface version
if ($version ne 2) {
	die "Unsupported query-fsmonitor hook version '$version'.\n" .
	    "Falling back to scanning...\n";
}

my $git_work_tree = get_working_dir();

my $retry = 1;

my $json_pkg;
eval {
	require JSON::XS;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::XS";
	1;
} or do {
	require JSON::PP;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::PP";
};

launch_watchman();

sub launch_watchman {
	my $o = watchman_query();
	if (is_work_tree_watched($o)) {
		output_result($o->{clock}, @{$o->{files}});
	}
}

sub output_result {
	my ($clockid, @files) = @_;

	# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
	# binmode $fh, ":utf8";
	# print $fh "$clockid\n@files\n";
	# close $fh;

	binmode STDOUT, ":utf8";
	print $clockid;
	print "\0";
	local $, = "\0";
	print @files;
}

sub watchman_clock {
	my $response = qx/watchman clock "$git_work_tree"/;
	die "Failed to get clock id on '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub watchman_query {
	my $pid = open2(\*CHLD_OUT, \*CHLD_IN, 'watchman -j --no-pretty')
	or die "open2() failed: $!\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n";

	# In the query expression below we're asking for names of files that
	# changed since $last_update_token but not from the .git folder.
	#
	# To accomplish this, we're using the "since" generator to use the
	# recency index to select candidate nodes and "fields" to limit the
	# output to file names only. Then we're using the "expression" term to
	# further constrain the results.
	my $last_update_line = "";
	if (substr($last_update_token, 0, 1) eq "c") {
		$last_update_token = "\"$last_update_token\"";
		$last_update_line = qq[\n"since": $last_update_token,];
	}
	my $query = <<"	END";
		["query", "$git_work_tree", {$last_update_line
			"fields": ["name"],
			"expression": ["not", ["dirname", ".git"]]
		}]
	END

	# Uncomment for debugging the watchman query
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-query.json");
	# print $fh $query;
	# close $fh;

	print CHLD_IN $query;
	close CHLD_IN;
	my $response = do {local $/; <CHLD_OUT>};

	# Uncomment for debugging the watch response
	# open ($fh, ">", ".git/watchman-response.json");
	# print $fh $response;
	# close $fh;

	die "Watchman: command returned no output.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $response eq "";
	die "Watchman: command returned invalid output: $response\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" unless $response =~ /^\{/;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub is_work_tree_watched {
	my ($output) = @_;
	my $error = $output->{error};
	if ($retry > 0 and $error and $error =~ m/unable to resolve root .* directory (.*) is not watched/) {
		$retry--;
		my $response = qx/watchman watch "$git_work_tree"/;
		die "Failed to make watchman watch '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		    "Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;
		$output = $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
		$error = $output->{error};
		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
		# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
		# close $fh;

		# Watchman will always return all files on the first query so
		# return the fast "everything is dirty" flag to git and do the
		# Watchman query just to get it over with now so we won't pay
		# the cost in git to look up each individual file.
		my $o = watchman_clock();
		$error = $output->{error};

		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		output_result($o->{clock}, ("/"));
		$last_update_token = $o->{clock};

		eval { launch_watchman() };
		return 0;
	}

	die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

	return 1;
}

sub get_working_dir {
	my $working_dir;
	if ($^O =~ 'msys' || $^O =~ 'cygwin') {
		$working_dir = Win32::GetCwd();
		$working_dir =~ tr/\\/\//;
	} else {
		require Cwd;
		$working_dir = Cwd::cwd();
	}

	return $working_dir;
}
#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to update a checked-out tree on a git push.
#
# This hook is invoked by git-receive-pack(1) when it reacts to git
# push and updates reference(s) in its repository, and when the push
# tries to update the branch that is currently checked out and the
# receive.denyCurrentBranch configuration variable is set to
# updateInstead.
#
# By default, such a push is refused if the working tree and the index
# of the remote repository has any difference from the currently
# checked out commit; when both the working tree and the index match
# the current commit, they are updated to match the newly pushed tip
# of the branch. This hook is to be used to override the default
# behaviour; however the code below reimplements the default behaviour
# as a starting point for convenient modification.
#
# The hook receives the commit with which the tip of the current
# branch is going to be updated:
commit=$1

# It can exit with a non-zero status to refuse the push (when it does
# so, it must not modify the index or the working tree).
die () {
	echo >&2 "$*"
	exit 1
}

# Or it can make any necessary changes to the working tree and to the
# index to bring them to the desired state when the tip of the current
# branch is updated to the new commit, and exit with a zero status.
#
# For example, the hook can simply run git read-tree -u -m HEAD "$1"
# in order to emulate git fetch that is run in the reverse direction
# with git push, as the two-tree form of git read-tree -u -m is
# essentially the same as git switch or git checkout that switches
# branches while keeping the local changes in the working tree that do
# not interfere with the difference between the branches.

# The below is a more-or-less exact translation to shell of the C code
# for the default behaviour for git's push-to-checkout hook defined in
# the push_to_deploy() function in builtin/receive-pack.c.
#
# Note that the hook will be executed from the repository directory,
# not from the working tree, so if you want to perform operations on
# the working tree, you will have to adapt your code accordingly, e.g.
# by adding "cd .." or using relative paths.

if ! git update-index -q --ignore-submodules --refresh
then
	die "Up-to-date check failed"
fi

if ! git diff-files --quiet --ignore-submodules --
then
	die "Working directory has unstaged changes"
fi

# This is a rough translation of:
#
#   head_has_history() ? "HEAD" : EMPTY_TREE_SHA1_HEX
if git cat-file -e HEAD 2>/dev/null
then
	head=HEAD
else
	head=$(git hash-object -t tree --stdin </dev/null)
fi

if ! git diff-index --quiet --cached --ignore-submodules $head --
then
	die "Working directory has staged changes"
fi

if ! git read-tree -u -m "$commit"
then
	die "Could not update working tree to new HEAD"
fi
#!/bin/sh
#
# Copyright (c) 2006, 2008 Junio C Hamano
#
# The "pre-rebase" hook is run just before "git rebase" starts doing
# its job, and can prevent the command from running by exiting with
# non-zero status.
#
# The hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- the upstream the series was forked from.
# $2 -- the branch being rebased (or empty when rebasing the current branch).
#
# This sample shows how to prevent topic branches that are already
# merged to 'next' branch from getting rebased, because allowing it
# would result in rebasing already published history.

publish=next
basebranch="$1"
if test "$#" = 2
then
	topic="refs/heads/$2"
else
	topic=`git symbolic-ref HEAD` ||
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt rebasing detached HEAD
fi

case "$topic" in
refs/heads/??/*)
	;;
*)
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt others.
	;;
esac

# Now we are dealing with a topic branch being rebased
# on top of master.  Is it OK to rebase it?

# Does the topic really exist?
git show-ref -q "$topic" || {
	echo >&2 "No such branch $topic"
	exit 1
}

# Is topic fully merged to master?
not_in_master=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^master "$topic"`
if test -z "$not_in_master"
then
	echo >&2 "$topic is fully merged to master; better remove it."
	exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
fi

# Is topic ever merged to next?  If so you should not be rebasing it.
only_next_1=`git rev-list ^master "^$topic" ${publish} | sort`
only_next_2=`git rev-list ^master           ${publish} | sort`
if test "$only_next_1" = "$only_next_2"
then
	not_in_topic=`git rev-list "^$topic" master`
	if test -z "$not_in_topic"
	then
		echo >&2 "$topic is already up to date with master"
		exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
	else
		exit 0
	fi
else
	not_in_next=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^${publish} "$topic"`
	/usr/bin/perl -e '
		my $topic = $ARGV[0];
		my $msg = "* $topic has commits already merged to public branch:\n";
		my (%not_in_next) = map {
			/^([0-9a-f]+) /;
			($1 => 1);
		} split(/\n/, $ARGV[1]);
		for my $elem (map {
				/^([0-9a-f]+) (.*)$/;
				[$1 => $2];
			} split(/\n/, $ARGV[2])) {
			if (!exists $not_in_next{$elem->[0]}) {
				if ($msg) {
					print STDERR $msg;
					undef $msg;
				}
				print STDERR " $elem->[1]\n";
			}
		}
	' "$topic" "$not_in_next" "$not_in_master"
	exit 1
fi

<<\DOC_END

This sample hook safeguards topic branches that have been
published from being rewound.

The workflow assumed here is:

 * Once a topic branch forks from "master", "master" is never
   merged into it again (either directly or indirectly).

 * Once a topic branch is fully cooked and merged into "master",
   it is deleted.  If you need to build on top of it to correct
   earlier mistakes, a new topic branch is created by forking at
   the tip of the "master".  This is not strictly necessary, but
   it makes it easier to keep your history simple.

 * Whenever you need to test or publish your changes to topic
   branches, merge them into "next" branch.

The script, being an example, hardcodes the publish branch name
to be "next", but it is trivial to make it configurable via
$GIT_DIR/config mechanism.

With this workflow, you would want to know:

(1) ... if a topic branch has ever been merged to "next".  Young
    topic branches can have stupid mistakes you would rather
    clean up before publishing, and things that have not been
    merged into other branches can be easily rebased without
    affecting other people.  But once it is published, you would
    not want to rewind it.

(2) ... if a topic branch has been fully merged to "master".
    Then you can delete it.  More importantly, you should not
    build on top of it -- other people may already want to
    change things related to the topic as patches against your
    "master", so if you need further changes, it is better to
    fork the topic (perhaps with the same name) afresh from the
    tip of "master".

Let's look at this example:

		   o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "next"
		  /       /           /           /
		 /   a---a---b A     /           /
		/   /               /           /
	       /   /   c---c---c---c B         /
	      /   /   /             \         /
	     /   /   /   b---b C     \       /
	    /   /   /   /             \     /
    ---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "master"


A, B and C are topic branches.

 * A has one fix since it was merged up to "next".

 * B has finished.  It has been fully merged up to "master" and "next",
   and is ready to be deleted.

 * C has not merged to "next" at all.

We would want to allow C to be rebased, refuse A, and encourage
B to be deleted.

To compute (1):

	git rev-list ^master ^topic next
	git rev-list ^master        next

	if these match, topic has not merged in next at all.

To compute (2):

	git rev-list master..topic

	if this is empty, it is fully merged to "master".

DOC_END
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to check the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with one argument, the name of the file
# that has the commit message.  The hook should exit with non-zero
# status after issuing an appropriate message if it wants to stop the
# commit.  The hook is allowed to edit the commit message file.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "commit-msg".

# Uncomment the below to add a Signed-off-by line to the message.
# Doing this in a hook is a bad idea in general, but the prepare-commit-msg
# hook is more suited to it.
#
# SOB=$(git var GIT_AUTHOR_IDENT | sed -n 's/^\(.*>\).*$/Signed-off-by: \1/p')
# grep -qs "^$SOB" "$1" || echo "$SOB" >> "$1"

# This example catches duplicate Signed-off-by lines.

test "" = "$(grep '^Signed-off-by: ' "$1" |
	 sort | uniq -c | sed -e '/^[ 	]*1[ 	]/d')" || {
	echo >&2 Duplicate Signed-off-by lines.
	exit 1
}
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to block unannotated tags from entering.
# Called by "git receive-pack" with arguments: refname sha1-old sha1-new
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "update".
#
# Config
# ------
# hooks.allowunannotated
#   This boolean sets whether unannotated tags will be allowed into the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletetag
#   This boolean sets whether deleting tags will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowmodifytag
#   This boolean sets whether a tag may be modified after creation. By default
#   it won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletebranch
#   This boolean sets whether deleting branches will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.denycreatebranch
#   This boolean sets whether remotely creating branches will be denied
#   in the repository.  By default this is allowed.
#

# --- Command line
refname="$1"
oldrev="$2"
newrev="$3"

# --- Safety check
if [ -z "$GIT_DIR" ]; then
	echo "Don't run this script from the command line." >&2
	echo " (if you want, you could supply GIT_DIR then run" >&2
	echo "  $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>)" >&2
	exit 1
fi

if [ -z "$refname" -o -z "$oldrev" -o -z "$newrev" ]; then
	echo "usage: $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>" >&2
	exit 1
fi

# --- Config
allowunannotated=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowunannotated)
allowdeletebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletebranch)
denycreatebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.denycreatebranch)
allowdeletetag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletetag)
allowmodifytag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowmodifytag)

# check for no description
projectdesc=$(sed -e '1q' "$GIT_DIR/description")
case "$projectdesc" in
"Unnamed repository"* | "")
	echo "*** Project description file hasn't been set" >&2
	exit 1
	;;
esac

# --- Check types
# if $newrev is 0000...0000, it's a commit to delete a ref.
zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')
if [ "$newrev" = "$zero" ]; then
	newrev_type=delete
else
	newrev_type=$(git cat-file -t $newrev)
fi

case "$refname","$newrev_type" in
	refs/tags/*,commit)
		# un-annotated tag
		short_refname=${refname##refs/tags/}
		if [ "$allowunannotated" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** The un-annotated tag, $short_refname, is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			echo "*** Use 'git tag [ -a | -s ]' for tags you want to propagate." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,delete)
		# delete tag
		if [ "$allowdeletetag" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tag is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,tag)
		# annotated tag
		if [ "$allowmodifytag" != "true" ] && git rev-parse $refname > /dev/null 2>&1
		then
			echo "*** Tag '$refname' already exists." >&2
			echo "*** Modifying a tag is not allowed in this repository." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,commit)
		# branch
		if [ "$oldrev" = "$zero" -a "$denycreatebranch" = "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Creating a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,delete)
		# delete branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,commit)
		# tracking branch
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,delete)
		# delete tracking branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tracking branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	*)
		# Anything else (is there anything else?)
		echo "*** Update hook: unknown type of update to ref $refname of type $newrev_type" >&2
		exit 1
		;;
esac

# --- Finished
exit 0
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to check the commit log message taken by
# applypatch from an e-mail message.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an
# appropriate message if it wants to stop the commit.  The hook is
# allowed to edit the commit message file.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "applypatch-msg".

. git-sh-setup
commitmsg="$(git rev-parse --git-path hooks/commit-msg)"
test -x "$commitmsg" && exec "$commitmsg" ${1+"$@"}
:
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to prepare a packed repository for use over
# dumb transports.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "post-update".

exec git update-server-info
#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed.
# Called by "git merge" with no arguments.  The hook should
# exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate message to
# stderr if it wants to stop the merge commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-merge-commit".

. git-sh-setup
test -x "$GIT_DIR/hooks/pre-commit" &&
        exec "$GIT_DIR/hooks/pre-commit"
:
#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to verify what is about to be pushed.  Called by "git
# push" after it has checked the remote status, but before anything has been
# pushed.  If this script exits with a non-zero status nothing will be pushed.
#
# This hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- Name of the remote to which the push is being done
# $2 -- URL to which the push is being done
#
# If pushing without using a named remote those arguments will be equal.
#
# Information about the commits which are being pushed is supplied as lines to
# the standard input in the form:
#
#   <local ref> <local oid> <remote ref> <remote oid>
#
# This sample shows how to prevent push of commits where the log message starts
# with "WIP" (work in progress).

remote="$1"
url="$2"

zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')

while read local_ref local_oid remote_ref remote_oid
do
	if test "$local_oid" = "$zero"
	then
		# Handle delete
		:
	else
		if test "$remote_oid" = "$zero"
		then
			# New branch, examine all commits
			range="$local_oid"
		else
			# Update to existing branch, examine new commits
			range="$remote_oid..$local_oid"
		fi

		# Check for WIP commit
		commit=$(git rev-list -n 1 --grep '^WIP' "$range")
		if test -n "$commit"
		then
			echo >&2 "Found WIP commit in $local_ref, not pushing"
			exit 1
		fi
	fi
done

exit 0
